Read: schema/change-passport.v1.json, tools/bm_passport.py (the generate subcommand, roughly lines 550 onward, plus its older deposit path), tools/bm_passport_validator.py, tools/test_bm_passport.py, schema/fixtures/, docs/PASSPORT.md, and the redaction policy export_column in tools/bm_store.py near line 5416. IS THIS REALLY READY? Attack specifically the classes Claude-family reviews under-weight: environment inheritance in subprocess calls, shell quoting, locale decode (text=True without encoding), SQLite transaction and locking behavior when the store is read while another process writes, two-process contention on the same store file, JSON edge cases (unicode, huge strings, duplicate keys), and path handling on machines that are not this one. Also answer four named questions: (1) generate passes raw=True to five store accessors, deliberately bypassing the export_column redaction policy, justified by the precedent of dump(raw=True) and because redacted placeholders would produce a passport that looks filled but carries withheld text; is that defensible for an artifact designed to travel to the assurance side, and what guardrail is missing? (2) generate for a project id absent from the store exits 0 and writes a passport whose field 4 carries only the generic defaults; should the missing project be named or refused? (3) the determinism promise is byte-identical output for same tree and same --now; name any input that silently breaks it (dict ordering from sqlite, filesystem ordering, git config, locale). (4) the additive generate subcommand dispatch on argv[0]; any way the old no-verb CLI and the new verb can shadow or corrupt each other? Structure your reply as numbered findings each with severity (BLOCKER, MAJOR, MINOR), the exact file and line, what breaks, and a one-line fix. If you find nothing in a class, say COULD NOT BREAK for that class. Finish with a one-line verdict: READY or NOT READY plus the blocking finding ids.

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""The change passport, producer half: what BrotherMode deposits at the seam.
     3	
     4	WHAT THE SEAM IS. The change passport is the single crossing between
     5	execution provenance and assurance (docs/specs/2026-08-15-change-passport-
     6	seam.md in the BrotherSBE repository is the contract). BrotherMode produces
     7	one, BrotherSBE consumes one, and it never travels back. This tool is the
     8	producing end and the only place BrotherMode writes passport data.
     9	
    10	THE TWO FIELDS THIS TOOL OWNS. Execution provenance owns exactly two of the
    11	five fields the chain names, because only execution can see them:
    12	
    13	  whoDidIt              the session and the claims it holds, plus the
    14	                         accountable person by name (never a role)
    15	  whereItCameFrom        the development method, named and not judged
    16	
    17	A third field rides along because execution is also the only side that
    18	knows what IT left undone, which assurance has no way to see:
    19	
    20	  whatWasNotEstablished  gaps this producer itself hit while assembling the
    21	                         deposit (a missing store, an unlabeled session, an
    22	                         accountable person nobody named)
    23	
    24	DIRECTION OF TRAVEL. This tool writes <root>/.sbe/passport.json and reads
    25	NOTHING under .sbe/: no tasks.json, no evidence/, no existing passport.json.
    26	Reaching across the seam to fill a field is the exact failure the seam
    27	exists to prevent. Its only inputs are tools/bm_store.py's own store (read
    28	only, never STATE.md, which is a generated view and not truth), `git config
    29	user.name`, and the --accountable / --method flags.
    30	
    31	A HOLLOW VALUE IS NOT A VALUE. An empty string, a whitespace-only string, an
    32	empty list, or null all read as absence on the consuming side. A field this
    33	tool cannot establish honestly is OMITTED from the deposit, never padded
    34	with an empty placeholder to look filled, and the omission is explained on
    35	stdout.
    36	
    37	NOT A GATE. This tool reports and deposits, it never blocks on what it
    38	FINDS: a store it could not read, a session nobody labeled and an
    39	accountable person nobody named are all exit 0 with the gap written into
    40	field 4, never a refusal. Three exits, stated exactly, because a tool whose
    41	own documentation misreports its exit codes is the same overclaim this
    42	product exists to catch:
    43	
    44	  0  a deposit was written, whatever it could and could not establish
    45	  1  the deposit itself could not be written to disk (the one case where
    46	     running this tool and getting nothing is itself the finding)
    47	  2  a usage error, or the NO-DATA verdict where no project root could be
    48	     resolved at all, which is not a finding about a change but a failure
    49	     to find a change to report on
    50	
    51	Python floor is 3.9: no match statements, no `X | Y` annotations. Standard
    52	library only, mirroring every other tool in this directory.
    53	
    54	Run it with --help for the usage line, which resolves the command to the
    55	layout the reader actually installed rather than naming a tools/ directory
    56	a packaged install does not have.
    57	"""
    58	import datetime
    59	import json
    60	import os
    61	import subprocess
    62	import sys
    63	
    64	HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    65	
    66	#: Written by the `generate` verb (see the section below build_deposit):
    67	#: the full five-field passport, schema/change-passport.v1.json's own
    68	#: shape, validated standalone by tools/bm_passport_validator.py. Distinct
    69	#: from the older no-verb "deposit" behavior above, which writes only the
    70	#: two fields execution provenance uniquely owns plus its own gap list.
    71	#: Kept side by side rather than merged: the deposit behavior above is
    72	#: already shipped, tested and wired into CI under its own CLI shape, and
    73	#: replacing it outright would drop a working, reviewed producer for an
    74	#: unproven rewrite. `generate` is additive; nothing about the no-verb
    75	#: path below changes.
    76	GENERATE_SCHEMA_MARKER = "change-passport/v1"
    77	
    78	#: Fixed by the contract: the producer always deposits here unless --out
    79	#: names a different path (used by this tool's own tests so they never
    80	#: touch a real .sbe/ directory).
    81	DEPOSIT_REL = os.path.join(".sbe", "passport.json")
    82	
    83	#: Named and not judged, per the contract. Always true of anything this
    84	#: tool produces a deposit for, so it is never hollow and never guessed;
    85	#: --method overrides it when a session ran a different flow on top.
    86	DEFAULT_METHOD = "BrotherMode"

    88	
    89	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    90	# Root resolution. Honors --root when given; otherwise borrows bm_store's
    91	# resolve_root() purely for that lookup. Mirrors tools/bm_idle.py's
    92	# resolve_project_root() exactly, since that file is this tool's structural
    93	# template.
    94	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    95	
    96	def _load_bm_store():
    97	    """Load bm_store.py by PATH: this tool is invoked with an arbitrary
    98	    cwd, and a plain `import bm_store` would depend on whichever sys.path
    99	    the caller happened to have. Never raises: an unimportable module
   100	    degrades to an explicit reason string, not a crash."""
   101	    try:
   102	        import importlib.util
   103	        path = os.path.join(HERE, "bm_store.py")
   104	        spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
   105	            "bm_store_for_passport", path)
   106	        if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
   107	            return None, "could not build an import spec for bm_store.py"
   108	        mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
   109	        spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
   110	        return mod, None
   111	    except Exception as exc:
   112	        return None, "%s: %s" % (type(exc).__name__, exc)
   113

   115	def resolve_project_root(explicit_root):
   116	    """Return (root, None) or (None, reason). `explicit_root` (--root)
   117	    always wins and is checked directly against the filesystem; nothing
   118	    else here reads bm_store at all in that case. Otherwise
   119	    bm_store.resolve_root() is borrowed, which returns a (path, source)
   120	    TUPLE that must be unpacked."""
   121	    if explicit_root:
   122	        candidate = os.path.realpath(os.path.expanduser(explicit_root))
   123	        if not os.path.isdir(candidate):
   124	            return None, "no such directory: %s" % candidate
   125	        return candidate, None
   126	    mod, err = _load_bm_store()
   127	    if mod is None:
   128	        return None, ("could not load bm_store.py to resolve the project "
   129	                      "root (%s); pass --root explicitly" % err)
   130	    root, _source = mod.resolve_root()
   131	    if not root:
   132	        return None, ("nothing anchors a BrotherMode project here (no "
   133	                      "BROTHERMODE_ROOT, no marker directory, no git repo "
   134	                      "found); pass --root explicitly")
   135	    return root, None

   138	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   139	# Hollow-value detection. Matches the consuming side's own definition
   140	# (BrotherSBE tools/sbe_onepager.py's answered()) exactly: '' and None carry
   141	# nothing, 0 and False are real answers, an empty list/dict carries nothing.
   142	# Defined here rather than imported, because the producer never reads a
   143	# BrotherSBE file at runtime; matching the CONTRACT'S definition by hand is
   144	# the whole point of a seam.
   145	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   146	
   147	def _answered(value):
   148	    if value is None:
   149	        return False
   150	    if isinstance(value, str):

   161	def _store_snapshot(root):
   162	    """Return (state, payload, store_path). state is one of 'absent',
   163	    'unreadable', 'ok'. payload is a plain-English reason for 'absent' and
   164	    'unreadable', or the dict from Store.dump(raw=True) for 'ok'.
   165	    store_path is bm_store's own path for the store file when it could be
   166	    computed, else None. Never raises: every exception this store can throw
   167	    on open or on dump is caught and turned into 'unreadable' rather than
   168	    left to crash this tool."""
   169	    mod, err = _load_bm_store()
   170	    if mod is None:
   171	        return "unreadable", "bm_store.py could not be loaded (%s)" % err, None
   172	    store_path = mod.store_path(root)
   173	    try:
   174	        store = mod.ReadOnlyStore(root)
   175	    except mod.OwnershipRefused as e:
   176	        if e.reason == "no-store":
   177	            return "absent", "no BrotherMode store at %s" % store_path, store_path
   178	        return ("unreadable",
   179	                "the BrotherMode store at %s could not be opened: %s"
   180	                % (store_path, e), store_path)
   181	    except mod.StoreCorrupt as e:
   182	        return ("unreadable",
   183	                "the BrotherMode store at %s is corrupt: %s" % (store_path, e),
   184	                store_path)
   185	    except Exception as e:
   186	        return ("unreadable",
   187	                "the BrotherMode store at %s could not be opened: %s: %s"
   188	                % (store_path, type(e).__name__, e), store_path)
   189	    try:
   190	        dump = store.dump(raw=True)
   191	    except Exception as e:
   192	        return ("unreadable",
   193	                "the BrotherMode store at %s could not be read: %s: %s"
   194	                % (store_path, type(e).__name__, e), store_path)
   195	    finally:
   196	        try:
   197	            store.close()
   198	        except Exception:
   199	            pass
   200	    return "ok", dump, store_path

   203	def _session_claim_lines(dump):
   204	    """Return (lines, total_files, skipped_records).
   205	
   206	    One line per distinct session holding an ACTIVE record, each naming the
   207	    files claimed across every active record that session holds. Sorted by
   208	    session label so output is deterministic run to run.
   209	
   210	    The two extra return values exist because of what this function DOES NOT
   211	    read, which an adversarial review found could produce a confidently wrong
   212	    field 2 with a clean field 4. `records.state` is one of active, parked,
   213	    complete or adopted, and only active is read here. A session deposits at
   214	    CLOSE, which is exactly when its own record is most likely already
   215	    complete or parked, so a deposit assembled from active claims alone can
   216	    name a concurrent, unrelated session as the author and say nothing was
   217	    missing. Counting the skipped records here is what lets build_deposit say
   218	    so in field 4 rather than stay silent. Same reason for the file total: a
   219	    session line reading "0 files" is not provenance, and the caller cannot
   220	    see that from the formatted strings."""
   221	    records = dump.get("records") or []
   222	    claims = dump.get("claims") or []
   223	    files_by_uuid = {}
   224	    for c in claims:
   225	        u = c.get("lifecycle_uuid")
   226	        files_by_uuid[u] = files_by_uuid.get(u, 0) + 1
   227	
   228	    totals = {}  # session label -> [file_count, record_count]
   229	    skipped = 0
   230	    for r in records:
   231	        if r.get("state") != "active":
   232	            skipped += 1
   233	            continue
   234	        sid = (r.get("session_id") or "").strip()
   235	        label = sid if sid else "(no session id recorded)"
   236	        entry = totals.setdefault(label, [0, 0])
   237	        entry[0] += files_by_uuid.get(r.get("lifecycle_uuid"), 0)
   238	        entry[1] += 1
   239	
   240	    lines = []
   241	    total_files = 0
   242	    for label in sorted(totals):
   243	        n_files, n_records = totals[label]
   244	        total_files += n_files
   245	        lines.append(
   246	            "session %s, holding claims on %d file%s across %d record%s"
   247	            % (label, n_files, "" if n_files == 1 else "s",
   248	               n_records, "" if n_records == 1 else "s"))
   249	    return lines, total_files, skipped

   267	def _git_user_name(root):
   268	    try:
   269	        result = subprocess.run(
   270	            ["git", "-C", root, "config", "user.name"],
   271	            stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
   272	            universal_newlines=True, timeout=5)
   273	    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
   274	        return None
   275	    if result.returncode != 0:
   276	        return None
   277	    name = result.stdout.strip()
   278	    return name or None
   279	
   280	
   281	def _accountable_line(root, explicit):
   282	    """Return (line_or_None, gap_reason_or_None).
   283	
   284	    A name taken from git STILL carries a gap reason, which is the whole
   285	    point of field 4. `git config user.name` resolves the machine's global
   286	    identity: it answers who owns this laptop, not who is accountable for
   287	    THIS change, and it answers even when `root` is not a repository at all.
   288	    Naming it without saying where it came from is the same overclaim as an
   289	    empty field 4 on a store that could not be read."""
   290	    if explicit and explicit.strip():
   291	        return "accountable: %s" % explicit.strip(), None
   292	    name = _git_user_name(root)
   293	    if name:
   294	        return ("accountable: %s" % name,
   295	                "the accountable person was INFERRED from `git config "
   296	                "user.name` at %s, which resolves the machine's global git "
   297	                "identity; nobody stated an accountable person for this "
   298	                "change (no --accountable flag was given)" % root)
   299	    return None, (
   300	        "the accountable person could not be established: no --accountable "
   301	        "flag was given and `git config user.name` returned nothing at %s"
   302	        % root)
   303	
   304	
   305	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   306	# Assembling the deposit.
   307	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   308	
   309	def build_deposit(root, accountable_flag, method_flag):
   310	    """Return (deposit_dict, not_established_lines, notes_for_stdout)."""
   311	    state, payload, store_path = _store_snapshot(root)
   312	
   313	    session_lines = []
   314	    not_established = []
   315	    if state == "ok":
   316	        session_lines, total_files, skipped = _session_claim_lines(payload)
   317	        if not session_lines:
   318	            not_established.append(
   319	                "no active claim was found in the BrotherMode store at %s, "
   320	                "so this producer cannot say which session or files this "
   321	                "change belongs to" % store_path)
   322	        if skipped:
   323	            not_established.append(
   324	                "%d record(s) in the BrotherMode store are not active "
   325	                "(complete, parked or adopted) and were NOT read, so a "
   326	                "session whose record already closed does not appear above; "
   327	                "only claims active at the moment this deposit was written "
   328	                "were read" % skipped)
   329	        if session_lines and total_files == 0:
   330	            not_established.append(
   331	                "the active record(s) claim no files at all, so this deposit "
   332	                "can name the session but cannot say which files the change "
   333	                "touched")
   334	        unlabeled = _unlabeled_active_count(payload)
   335	        if unlabeled:
   336	            not_established.append(
   337	                "%d active record(s) in the BrotherMode store carry no "
   338	                "session id, so their claims cannot be attributed to a "
   339	                "session" % unlabeled)
   340	    elif state == "absent":
   341	        not_established.append(
   342	            "%s, so session and claim history for this change could not be "
   343	            "established" % payload)
   344	    else:
   345	        not_established.append(
   346	            "%s, so session and claim history for this change could not be "
   347	            "read" % payload)
   348	
   349	    accountable_line, accountable_gap = _accountable_line(root, accountable_flag)
   350	    if accountable_gap:
   351	        not_established.append(accountable_gap)
   352	
   353	    who_did_it = list(session_lines)
   354	    if accountable_line:
   355	        who_did_it.append(accountable_line)
   356	
   357	    # Always true of this tool, never fabricated: it is a structural limit
   358	    # of what an execution-side deposit can attest to, and it is the reason
   359	    # whatWasNotEstablished can never legitimately read as empty even on a
   360	    # perfectly healthy store.
   361	    not_established.append(
   362	        "this deposit reports BrotherMode's own store only (session ids "
   363	        "and claimed files); it does not know whether the work was "
   364	        "reviewed, tested, or verified, which is assurance's job to "
   365	        "establish, not execution's")
   366	
   367	    method = method_flag.strip() if (method_flag and method_flag.strip()) else DEFAULT_METHOD
   368	
   369	    deposit = {}
   370	    if _answered(who_did_it):
   371	        deposit["whoDidIt"] = who_did_it
   372	    if _answered(method):
   373	        deposit["whereItCameFrom"] = method
   374	    if _answered(not_established):
   375	        deposit["whatWasNotEstablished"] = not_established
   376	
   377	    notes = ["store: %s%s" % (state, "" if state == "ok" else " (%s)" % payload)]
   378	    for field in ("whoDidIt", "whereItCameFrom", "whatWasNotEstablished"):
   379	        notes.append("%s: %s" % (field, "carried" if field in deposit
   380	                                        else "NOT established, omitted"))
   381	    return deposit, not_established, notes
   382	
   383	
   384	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   385	# `generate`: the full five-field passport (schema/change-passport.v1.json).
   386	#
   387	# Reads ONLY the five ReadOnlyStore accessors named in the task brief
   388	# (get_project, list_tasks, list_evidence, list_attribution,
   389	# open_key_decisions), plus git (subprocess, explicit failure paths) and
   390	# the --accountable / --method / --now flags. Never touches the fence
   391	# tables (records, claims) the no-verb deposit above reads through
   392	# store.dump(); "who did it" is rebuilt here from the attribution log
   393	# instead, which is one of the five allowed surfaces.
   394	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   395	
   396	#: Default gap lines, one per keyword. A category is DROPPED only when that
   397	#: keyword (or its space-separated spelling) turns up in a task title or an
   398	#: evidence row's kind/ref/note, i.e. the store shows it was actually
   399	#: examined. This is a heuristic over free text, disclosed as one here and
   400	#: in docs/PASSPORT.md, not a claim of certainty.
   401	_DEFAULT_GAP_KEYWORDS = (
   402	    ("regression", "no regression pass is recorded in the store for this change"),
   403	    ("performance", "no performance measurement is recorded in the store for this change"),
   404	    ("cross-device", "no cross-device check is recorded in the store for this change"),
   405	    ("interface", "no interface behaviour review is recorded in the store for this change"),
   406	    ("translat", "the translated copy was not read by anyone who speaks the "
   407	                 "language, per this record"),
   408	)
   409	
   410	
   411	def _run_git(root, args):
   412	    """(stdout_or_None, error_or_None). Never raises: a missing git binary,
   413	    a bad ref, or a non-repository root all become a plain-English reason
   414	    string, never a traceback and never a silently empty result."""
   415	    try:
   416	        result = subprocess.run(
   417	            ["git", "-C", root] + list(args),
   418	            stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
   419	            text=True, encoding="utf-8", timeout=30)
   420	    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as e:
   421	        return None, "%s: %s" % (type(e).__name__, e)
   422	    if result.returncode != 0:
   423	        return None, (result.stderr.strip() or
   424	                      "git %s exited %d" % (" ".join(args), result.returncode))
   425	    return result.stdout, None
   426	
   427	
   428	def _repo_identity(root):
   429	    """The origin remote URL, or the directory name when there is none.
   430	    Never raises and never returns an empty string: a directory name is
   431	    always available as the last resort."""
   432	    out, _err = _run_git(root, ["remote", "get-url", "origin"])
   433	    if out and out.strip():
   434	        return out.strip()
   435	    return os.path.basename(os.path.abspath(root).rstrip(os.sep)) or root
   436	
   437	
   438	def _files_touched(root, base, head):
   439	    """(sorted_files, gap_reason_or_None). A git failure is reported as a
   440	    NAMED gap and an empty list, never a silent empty list with no
   441	    explanation (the design law this function exists to satisfy)."""
   442	    out, err = _run_git(root, ["diff", "--name-only", "%s..%s" % (base, head)])
   443	    if err is not None:
   444	        return [], ("git diff --name-only %s..%s failed: %s, so the files "
   445	                    "touched by this change could not be established"
   446	                    % (base, head, err))
   447	    files = sorted(set(line.strip() for line in out.splitlines() if line.strip()))
   448	    return files, None
   449	
   450	
   451	def _open_readonly_store(root):
   452	    """(store_or_None, state, reason). state is one of 'ok', 'absent',
   453	    'unreadable'. The caller is responsible for closing a returned store.
   454	    Mirrors _store_snapshot's three-state shape above so both verbs report
   455	    an absent or corrupt store the same honest way, without sharing code
   456	    that would otherwise open the store TWICE for one invocation."""
   457	    mod, err = _load_bm_store()
   458	    if mod is None:
   459	        return None, "unreadable", "bm_store.py could not be loaded (%s)" % err
   460	    store_path = mod.store_path(root)
   461	    try:
   462	        store = mod.ReadOnlyStore(root)
   463	    except mod.OwnershipRefused as e:
   464	        if e.reason == "no-store":
   465	            return None, "absent", "no BrotherMode store at %s" % store_path
   466	        return None, "unreadable", ("the BrotherMode store at %s could not "
   467	                                    "be opened: %s" % (store_path, e))
   468	    except mod.StoreCorrupt as e:
   469	        return None, "unreadable", ("the BrotherMode store at %s is "
   470	                                    "corrupt: %s" % (store_path, e))
   471	    except Exception as e:
   472	        return None, "unreadable", ("the BrotherMode store at %s could not "
   473	                                    "be opened: %s: %s"
   474	                                    % (store_path, type(e).__name__, e))
   475	    return store, "ok", None
   476	
   477	
   478	def _session_lines(attribution_rows, tasks_by_id):
   479	    """Every session named on the attribution log for this project, sorted
   480	    by session id, each with the claims (output artifacts, or else the
   481	    task it touched, or else the action name) it can be shown to hold.
   482	    Returns a list of {"label", "claims"} dicts, schema/change-
   483	    passport.v1.json's own shape for details.who.sessions."""
   484	    groups = {}
   485	    for row in attribution_rows:
   486	        sid = (row.get("session_id") or "").strip()
   487	        if not sid:
   488	            continue
   489	        claims = groups.setdefault(sid, set())
   490	        artifacts = [a for a in (row.get("output_artifacts") or []) if a]
   491	        if artifacts:
   492	            for artifact in artifacts:
   493	                claims.add(str(artifact))
   494	        else:
   495	            task_id = row.get("task_id")
   496	            if task_id and task_id in tasks_by_id:
   497	                claims.add(tasks_by_id[task_id].get("title") or task_id)
   498	            elif row.get("action"):
   499	                claims.add(str(row["action"]))
   500	    return [{"label": sid, "claims": sorted(groups[sid])} for sid in sorted(groups)]
   501	
   502	
   503	def _resolve_accountable(root, explicit, attribution_rows):
   504	    """(name_or_None, gap_reason_or_None). Three sources in order:
   505	    --accountable, the newest human actor on the attribution log (already
   506	    newest-first per list_attribution's own ORDER BY), `git config
   507	    user.name`. None for both means every source came up empty; the
   508	    caller turns that into the hard generation error the design names."""
   509	    if explicit and explicit.strip():
   510	        return explicit.strip(), None
   511	    for row in attribution_rows:
   512	        name = (row.get("actor_name") or "").strip()
   513	        if row.get("actor_type") == "human" and name:
   514	            return name, ("the accountable person was INFERRED from the "
   515	                          "newest human attribution event recorded for this "
   516	                          "project; no --accountable flag was given")
   517	    name = _git_user_name(root)
   518	    if name:
   519	        return name, ("the accountable person was INFERRED from `git "
   520	                      "config user.name` at %s, which resolves the "
   521	                      "machine's global git identity; no --accountable flag "
   522	                      "was given and the store named no human actor" % root)
   523	    return None, None
   524	
   525	
   526	def _method_name(method_flag, decision_rows):
   527	    """(name, was_defaulted). --method wins outright. Otherwise the newest
   528	    open DECISION insight whose decision_class is "method" (open_key_
   529	    decisions is already newest-first) supplies the name the store itself
   530	    recorded. Only the hard-coded default is used when neither exists."""
   531	    if method_flag and method_flag.strip():
   532	        return method_flag.strip(), False
   533	    for row in decision_rows:
   534	        if (row.get("decision_class") or "").strip().lower() == "method":
   535	            candidate = (row.get("subject") or row.get("claim") or "").strip()
   536	            if candidate:
   537	                return candidate, False
   538	    return "no method plugin detected; native flow", True
   539	
   540	
   541	def _evidence_entries(evidence_pairs):
   542	    """[(task_dict, entry_dict), ...] -> details.evidence entries. Origin
   543	    is always "local": the BrotherMode evidence table (kind/ref/note) has
   544	    no column recording whether a build system produced a check, unlike
   545	    BrotherSBE's own receipts (ciRunId). Reported honestly as a named gap
   546	    by the caller rather than guessed per entry."""
   547	    entries = []
   548	    for _task, row in evidence_pairs:
   549	        command = (row.get("ref") or row.get("kind") or "").strip() or "(unspecified check)"
   550	        verdict = (row.get("note") or "").strip() or "(no verdict recorded)"
   551	        entries.append({
   552	            "command": command,
   553	            "verdict": verdict,
   554	            "origin": "local",
   555	            "timestamp": row.get("created_at") or "",
   556	        })
   557	    return entries
   558	
   559	
   560	def _examined_text_blob(tasks, evidence_pairs):
   561	    parts = [str(t.get("title") or "") for t in tasks]
   562	    for _task, row in evidence_pairs:
   563	        parts.append(str(row.get("kind") or ""))
   564	        parts.append(str(row.get("ref") or ""))
   565	        parts.append(str(row.get("note") or ""))
   566	    return " ".join(parts).lower()
   567	
   568	
   569	def _default_gap_lines(tasks, evidence_pairs):
   570	    blob = _examined_text_blob(tasks, evidence_pairs)
   571	    lines = []
   572	    for keyword, sentence in _DEFAULT_GAP_KEYWORDS:
   573	        if keyword in blob or keyword.replace("-", " ") in blob:
   574	            continue
   575	        lines.append(sentence)
   576	    return lines
   577	
   578	
   579	def _canonical_now(now_arg):
   580	    """(iso_string_or_None, error_or_None). Canonicalises to seconds-
   581	    precision UTC ("...Z") whether the caller passed --now or not, so two
   582	    invocations with the SAME --now produce byte-identical output
   583	    regardless of the offset form given (a trailing Z, a numeric offset,
   584	    or none at all)."""
   585	    if now_arg:
   586	        text = now_arg
   587	        if text.endswith("Z"):
   588	            text = text[:-1] + "+00:00"
   589	        try:
   590	            parsed = datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(text)
   591	        except ValueError as e:
   592	            return None, "invalid --now value %r: %s" % (now_arg, e)
   593	        if parsed.tzinfo is None:
   594	            parsed = parsed.replace(tzinfo=datetime.timezone.utc)
   595	        parsed = parsed.astimezone(datetime.timezone.utc)
   596	    else:
   597	        parsed = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc)
   598	    return parsed.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"), None
   599	
   600	
   601	def build_passport(root, project_id, base, head, accountable_flag, method_flag,
   602	                   now_arg):
   603	    """(passport_dict_or_None, error_or_None). error set means generation
   604	    refused outright (only the empty-accountable law does this); every
   605	    other gap is folded into details.notEstablished / whatWasNotEstablished
   606	    instead, per the design's "reports, does not block" rule."""
   607	    now_str, now_err = _canonical_now(now_arg)
   608	    if now_err:
   609	        return None, now_err
   610	
   611	    repo = _repo_identity(root)
   612	    files, files_gap = _files_touched(root, base, head)
   613	
   614	    store, state, reason = _open_readonly_store(root)
   615	    tasks, attribution, decisions, evidence_pairs = [], [], [], []
   616	    store_gap = None
   617	    if state == "ok":
   618	        try:
   619	            # raw=True on every one of the five allowed accessors, on
   620	            # purpose: their default (raw=False) applies bm_store.py's own
   621	            # founder-text scrubber (_export_row), which withholds free text
   622	            # as "[WITHHELD: N chars of founder text]". That is the right
   623	            # default for an untrusted dump, but a passport's whole job is
   624	            # to carry REAL session labels, task titles and check commands;
   625	            # a redacted placeholder in whoDidIt or whatWasRun is not a
   626	            # privacy win, it is a passport that lies by looking filled.
   627	            # Precedent: the no-verb deposit path above already reads
   628	            # store.dump(raw=True) for the identical reason, and raw=True
   629	            # opens "no new disclosure surface beyond what dump(raw=True)
   630	            # already grants" per _export_row's own docstring.
   631	            project = store.get_project(project_id, raw=True)
   632	            tasks = store.list_tasks(project_id, raw=True)
   633	            attribution = store.list_attribution(project_id, limit=500, raw=True)
   634	            decisions = store.open_key_decisions(project_id, raw=True)
   635	            for task in tasks:
   636	                for row in store.list_evidence("task", task["task_id"], raw=True):
   637	                    evidence_pairs.append((task, row))
   638	            if project is None:
   639	                store_gap = ("no project %r was found in the BrotherMode "
   640	                             "store, so its tasks, evidence and decisions "
   641	                             "could not be read" % project_id)
   642	        finally:
   643	            store.close()
   644	    else:
   645	        store_gap = "%s, so session, evidence and decision history for this change could not be read" % reason
   646	
   647	    tasks_by_id = {t["task_id"]: t for t in tasks}
   648	    sessions = _session_lines(attribution, tasks_by_id)
   649	    accountable_name, accountable_gap = _resolve_accountable(
   650	        root, accountable_flag, attribution)
   651	    if accountable_name is None:
   652	        return None, ("no accountable human could be established: no "
   653	                      "--accountable flag was given, the store named no "
   654	                      "human actor, and `git config user.name` returned "
   655	                      "nothing at %s" % root)
   656	
   657	    method_name, method_defaulted = _method_name(method_flag, decisions)
   658	    evidence_entries = _evidence_entries(evidence_pairs)
   659	
   660	    items = list(_default_gap_lines(tasks, evidence_pairs))
   661	    for gap in (files_gap, store_gap, accountable_gap):
   662	        if gap:
   663	            items.append(gap)
   664	    if evidence_entries:
   665	        items.append(
   666	            "evidence origin (build system vs laptop) is not tracked by "
   667	            "the BrotherMode store's own evidence table; every entry above "
   668	            "is reported as local until that distinction exists")
   669	    justification = None
   670	    if not items:
   671	        justification = ("every default gap category was found examined in "
   672	                         "the store's own task and evidence text, and no "
   673	                         "other gap was detected by this generator")
   674	
   675	    what_was_done = ["change in %s, commit range %s..%s" % (repo, base, head)]
   676	    if files:
   677	        what_was_done.append(
   678	            "%d file(s) touched: %s" % (len(files), ", ".join(files)))
   679	    else:
   680	        what_was_done.append(
   681	            "no files were touched between %s and %s" % (base, head))
   682	
   683	    who_did_it = ["accountable: %s" % accountable_name]
   684	    for session in sessions:
   685	        claims = ", ".join(session["claims"]) if session["claims"] else "(no claim named)"
   686	        who_did_it.append("session %s: claims %s" % (session["label"], claims))
   687	
   688	    if evidence_entries:
   689	        what_was_run = [
   690	            "`%s` returned %s (%s, %s)" % (
   691	                e["command"], e["verdict"], e["origin"], e["timestamp"])
   692	            for e in evidence_entries]
   693	    else:
   694	        what_was_run = [
   695	            "no verification evidence is recorded in the BrotherMode store "
   696	            "for project %r" % project_id]
   697	
   698	    where_it_came_from = [method_name]
   699	    what_was_not_established = items if items else [justification]
   700	
   701	    passport = {
   702	        "schema": GENERATE_SCHEMA_MARKER,
   703	        "generatedAt": now_str,
   704	        "whatWasDone": what_was_done,
   705	        "whoDidIt": who_did_it,
   706	        "whatWasRun": what_was_run,
   707	        "whatWasNotEstablished": what_was_not_established,
   708	        "whereItCameFrom": where_it_came_from,
   709	        "change": {
   710	            "repo": repo,
   711	            "baseCommit": base,
   712	            "headCommit": head,
   713	            "filesTouched": files,
   714	        },
   715	        "details": {
   716	            "who": {"sessions": sessions, "accountableHuman": accountable_name},
   717	            "evidence": evidence_entries,
   718	            "method": {"name": method_name},
   719	            "notEstablished": {"items": items, "noneClaimJustification": justification},
   720	        },
   721	    }
   722	    return passport, None
   723	
   724	
   725	def _usage_generate():
   726	    cmd = None
   727	    mod, _err = _load_bm_store()
   728	    if mod is not None:
   729	        try:
   730	            cmd = mod.invocation("bm_passport.py", __file__)
   731	        except Exception:
   732	            cmd = None
   733	    if not cmd:
   734	        cmd = "python3 %s" % os.path.abspath(__file__)
   735	    return ("Usage: %s generate --project-id ID --base SHA --head SHA\n"
   736	            "         [--accountable NAME] [--method NAME] [--now ISO8601]\n"
   737	            "         [--out PATH] [--root DIR]\n\n"
   738	            "Writes the full five-field change passport (schema/change-"
   739	            "passport.v1.json)\nto stdout, or to --out when given. Exit 0 "
   740	            "on a written passport, whatever it\ncould and could not "
   741	            "establish. Exit 1 when no accountable human could be\n"
   742	            "established, or when --out could not be written. Exit 2 for a "
   743	            "usage error\nor an unresolvable project root." % cmd)
   744	
   745	
   746	def _parse_generate_argv(argv):
   747	    """(args_dict_or_None, error_or_None). error == 'help' means print
   748	    generate usage and exit 0."""
   749	    values = {"project_id": None, "base": None, "head": None,
   750	             "accountable": None, "method": None, "now": None,
   751	             "out": None, "root": None}
   752	    flag_to_key = {
   753	        "--project-id": "project_id", "--base": "base", "--head": "head",
   754	        "--accountable": "accountable", "--method": "method",
   755	        "--now": "now", "--out": "out", "--root": "root",
   756	    }
   757	    args = list(argv)
   758	    i = 0
   759	    while i < len(args):
   760	        arg = args[i]
   761	        if arg in ("--help", "-h"):
   762	            return None, "help"
   763	        if arg in flag_to_key:
   764	            if i + 1 >= len(args):
   765	                return None, "bm_passport generate: %s requires a value" % arg
   766	            values[flag_to_key[arg]] = args[i + 1]
   767	            i += 2
   768	        else:
   769	            return None, "bm_passport generate: unknown argument: %s" % arg
   770	    missing = [flag for flag, key in
   771	              (("--project-id", "project_id"), ("--base", "base"), ("--head", "head"))
   772	              if not (values[key] or "").strip()]
   773	    if missing:
   774	        return None, ("bm_passport generate: missing required argument(s): "
   775	                      "%s" % ", ".join(missing))
   776	    return values, None
   777	
   778	
   779	def _run_generate(argv):
   780	    values, err = _parse_generate_argv(argv)
   781	    if err == "help":
   782	        sys.stdout.write(_usage_generate() + "\n")
   783	        return 0
   784	    if err:
   785	        sys.stderr.write(err + "\n")
   786	        sys.stderr.write(_usage_generate() + "\n")
   787	        return 2
   788	
   789	    root, reason = resolve_project_root(values["root"])
   790	    if root is None:
   791	        sys.stdout.write("NO-DATA: %s\n" % reason)
   792	        return 2
   793	
   794	    passport, error = build_passport(
   795	        root, values["project_id"], values["base"], values["head"],
   796	        values["accountable"], values["method"], values["now"])
   797	    if error:
   798	        sys.stderr.write("bm-passport generate: %s\n" % error)
   799	        return 1
   800	
   801	    text = json.dumps(passport, sort_keys=True, indent=2,
   802	                      ensure_ascii=False) + "\n"
   803	    if not values["out"]:
   804	        sys.stdout.write(text)
   805	        return 0
   806	    try:
   807	        out_path = values["out"]
   808	        parent = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(out_path))
   809	        if parent and not os.path.isdir(parent):
   810	            os.makedirs(parent)
   811	        tmp_path = out_path + ".tmp"
   812	        with open(tmp_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   813	            fh.write(text)
   814	        os.replace(tmp_path, out_path)
   815	    except OSError as e:
   816	        sys.stderr.write("bm-passport generate: could not write %s (%s)\n"
   817	                         % (values["out"], e))
   818	        return 1
   819	    return 0
   820	
   821	
   822	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   823	# CLI.
   824	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   825	
   826	def _usage():
   827	    """The command a reader can actually paste, in the layout they have.
   828	
   829	    P17 put the shipping commands on PATH, so a hardcoded
   830	    `python3 tools/bm_passport.py` names a directory a packaged install does
   831	    not have. Resolved through bm_store.invocation(), the same resolver
   832	    tools/bm_handover.py and tools/bm_docs.py use, and swept for by
   833	    test_bm_store.py's TestP17InstructionTextMatchesTheInstalledLayout.
   834	    Degrades to this module's own absolute path, which is correct from any
   835	    cwd, when bm_store cannot be loaded: a usage string is not worth a
   836	    traceback."""
   837	    cmd = None
   838	    mod, _err = _load_bm_store()
   839	    if mod is not None:
   840	        try:
   841	            cmd = mod.invocation("bm_passport.py", __file__)
   842	        except Exception:
   843	            cmd = None
   844	    if not cmd:
   845	        cmd = "python3 %s" % os.path.abspath(__file__)
   846	    return ("Usage: %s [--root DIR] [--out PATH]\n"
   847	            "         [--accountable NAME] [--method TEXT]\n\n"
   848	            "Writes the change passport's producer deposit at <root>/%s.\n"
   849	            "Exit 0 when a deposit was written, whatever it could and could "
   850	            "not\nestablish. Exit 1 when the deposit itself could not be "
   851	            "written.\nExit 2 for a usage error, and for a NO-DATA verdict "
   852	            "where no project\nroot could be resolved at all, which is not a "
   853	            "finding about a change\nbut a failure to find one." % (
   854	                cmd, DEPOSIT_REL))
   855	
   856	
   857	def _parse_argv(argv):
   858	    """Return (root, out, accountable, method, error). `error` set to
   859	    'help' means print usage and exit 0; any other string means a plain
   860	    usage failure (exit 2)."""
   861	    args = list(argv)
   862	    root = None
   863	    out = None
   864	    accountable = None
   865	    method = None
   866	    i = 0
   867	    while i < len(args):
   868	        arg = args[i]
   869	        if arg in ("--help", "-h"):
   870	            return None, None, None, None, "help"
   871	        # An EMPTY value for the two path flags is refused rather than
   872	        # treated as absent. `--out "$OUT"` with an unset variable used to
   873	        # fall back to the project's real .sbe/passport.json, so a caller
   874	        # scripting a throwaway deposit silently wrote over the live one.
   875	        # The two text flags keep degrading quietly: an empty --accountable
   876	        # omits the field, which is honest, and an empty --method takes the
   877	        # default. Only the flags that decide WHERE bytes land are strict.
   878	        if arg == "--root":
   879	            if i + 1 >= len(args):
   880	                return None, None, None, None, "bm_passport: --root requires a value"
   881	            if not args[i + 1].strip():
   882	                return None, None, None, None, (
   883	                    "bm_passport: --root was given an empty value; omit the "
   884	                    "flag to resolve the project root automatically")
   885	            root = args[i + 1]
   886	            i += 2
   887	        elif arg == "--out":
   888	            if i + 1 >= len(args):
   889	                return None, None, None, None, "bm_passport: --out requires a value"
   890	            if not args[i + 1].strip():
   891	                return None, None, None, None, (
   892	                    "bm_passport: --out was given an empty value; omit the "
   893	                    "flag to write the deposit at <root>/%s" % DEPOSIT_REL)
   894	            out = args[i + 1]
   895	            i += 2
   896	        elif arg == "--accountable":
   897	            if i + 1 >= len(args):
   898	                return None, None, None, None, "bm_passport: --accountable requires a value"
   899	            accountable = args[i + 1]
   900	            i += 2
   901	        elif arg == "--method":
   902	            if i + 1 >= len(args):
   903	                return None, None, None, None, "bm_passport: --method requires a value"
   904	            method = args[i + 1]
   905	            i += 2
   906	        else:
   907	            return None, None, None, None, "bm_passport: unknown argument: %s" % arg
   908	    return root, out, accountable, method, None
   909	
   910	
   911	def _run(argv):
   912	    root_arg, out_arg, accountable_arg, method_arg, err = _parse_argv(argv)
   913	    if err == "help":
   914	        sys.stdout.write(_usage() + "\n")
   915	        return 0
   916	    if err:
   917	        sys.stderr.write(err + "\n")
   918	        sys.stderr.write(_usage() + "\n")
   919	        return 2
   920	
   921	    root, reason = resolve_project_root(root_arg)
   922	    if root is None:
   923	        sys.stdout.write("NO-DATA: %s\n" % reason)
   924	        return 2
   925	
   926	    deposit, _not_established, notes = build_deposit(root, accountable_arg, method_arg)
   927	    out_path = out_arg or os.path.join(root, DEPOSIT_REL)
   928	
   929	    # Written to a temp file and moved into place, the same idiom as
   930	    # tools/bm_docs.py and nine other tools here. Truncating the real path
   931	    # first means a failure mid-write leaves a half-written deposit, and the
   932	    # consumer classifies a truncated deposit as CORRUPT rather than absent,
   933	    # which is a worse answer than never having written one.
   934	    try:
   935	        parent = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(out_path))
   936	        if parent and not os.path.isdir(parent):
   937	            os.makedirs(parent)
   938	        tmp_path = out_path + ".tmp"
   939	        with open(tmp_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   940	            json.dump(deposit, fh, indent=2, sort_keys=True)
   941	            fh.write("\n")
   942	        os.replace(tmp_path, out_path)
   943	    except OSError as e:
   944	        sys.stderr.write("bm-passport: could not write %s (%s)\n" % (out_path, e))
   945	        return 1
   946	
   947	    sys.stdout.write("deposit written: %s\n" % out_path)
   948	    for line in notes:
   949	        sys.stdout.write(line + "\n")
   950	    return 0
   951	
   952	
   953	def main(argv):
   954	    try:
   955	        # "generate" is a new, additive verb (see build_passport above): the
   956	        # full five-field passport, a different CLI shape from the no-verb
   957	        # deposit behavior below it. Any OTHER first argument, flag or bare
   958	        # word, falls through to the original parser unchanged, which is
   959	        # what already refuses a stray positional argument as "unknown
   960	        # argument"; no existing invocation shape changes.
   961	        if argv and argv[0] == "generate":
   962	            return _run_generate(argv[1:])
   963	        return _run(argv)
   964	    except Exception as exc:
   965	        sys.stdout.write("NO-DATA: %s: %s\n" % (type(exc).__name__, exc))
   966	        return 2
   967	
   968	
   969	if __name__ == "__main__":
   970	    sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Standalone validator for a change passport v1 document
     3	(schema/change-passport.v1.json; docs/PASSPORT.md is the plain-language
     4	guide; docs/NORTH-STAR-CHAIN.md, section "The change passport, in full", is
     5	the spec authority).
     6	
     7	WHY STANDALONE. A third party (BrotherSBE, a CI runner, anyone who never
     8	installed this repository) needs to check a passport file without pulling in
     9	this project's own tooling or a jsonschema library. So this file imports
    10	NOTHING from this repository and NOTHING beyond the Python standard library.
    11	It re-implements the handful of rules that actually matter by hand, rather
    12	than deferring to the JSON Schema document, because a schema-only check
    13	cannot express "field 4's items may be empty only when a justification is
    14	given" without machinery this file is deliberately avoiding.
    15	
    16	WHAT IT CHECKS, each one a NAMED reason on its own line when it fails:
    17	
    18	  1. "schema" equals the literal string "change-passport/v1".
    19	  2. The five consumer keys (whatWasDone, whoDidIt, whatWasRun,
    20	     whatWasNotEstablished, whereItCameFrom) are present and ANSWERED: a
    21	     non-empty list whose entries are non-blank strings. Matches the
    22	     consuming side's own answered() (BrotherSBE tools/sbe_onepager.py):
    23	     '' and None carry nothing, an empty list carries nothing.
    24	  3. Field 4's law: details.notEstablished.items is non-empty, OR items is
    25	     empty and details.notEstablished.noneClaimJustification is a non-blank
    26	     string. A literal claim that nothing was left unestablished needs its
    27	     own justification.
    28	  4. change.baseCommit and change.headCommit are present and hex-shaped
    29	     (7 to 40 hex characters).
    30	  5. details.who.accountableHuman is a non-blank string. Accountability is a
    31	     name, never a role.
    32	  6. Every entry in details.evidence carries an origin in {"local", "ci"}
    33	     and a timestamp.
    34	
    35	Exit codes: 0 and the line "VALID" when every check passes. 1 and one line
    36	per violation when the document is well-formed JSON but fails a rule.
    37	2 and one line naming the problem when the file cannot be read or is not
    38	valid JSON. A crash is never a pass: any unexpected exception is caught and
    39	reported as a named error at exit 2.
    40	
    41	Python 3.9, standard library only, no third-party schema library.
    42	
    43	Usage: python3 tools/bm_passport_validator.py <file>
    44	"""
    45	import json
    46	import re
    47	import sys
    48	
    49	_COMMIT_RE = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-fA-F]{7,40}$")
    50	_SCHEMA_VALUE = "change-passport/v1"
    51	
    52	#: The five keys the consumer (BrotherSBE tools/sbe_passport.py) reads
    53	#: directly off the deposit, in the chain's own order.
    54	_CONSUMER_KEYS = (
    55	    (1, "whatWasDone"),
    56	    (2, "whoDidIt"),
    57	    (3, "whatWasRun"),
    58	    (4, "whatWasNotEstablished"),
    59	    (5, "whereItCameFrom"),
    60	)
    61	
    62	
    63	def _answered_string(value):
    64	    return isinstance(value, str) and value.strip() != ""
    65	
    66	
    67	def _answered_list_of_strings(value):
    68	    """True when `value` is a non-empty list whose every entry is a
    69	    non-blank string. Mirrors the consuming side's answered() for a list:
    70	    non-empty AND every element itself answered."""
    71	    if not isinstance(value, list) or not value:
    72	        return False
    73	    return all(_answered_string(v) for v in value)
    74	
    75	
    76	def validate(doc):
    77	    """Return a list of violation strings. Empty list means valid. `doc`
    78	    is assumed to already be parsed JSON (any type); every shape check is
    79	    done here rather than assumed."""
    80	    problems = []
    81	    if not isinstance(doc, dict):
    82	        return ["the document is not a JSON object"]
    83	
    84	    # 1. schema key.
    85	    schema_value = doc.get("schema")
    86	    if schema_value != _SCHEMA_VALUE:
    87	        problems.append(
    88	            "schema must equal %r, got %r" % (_SCHEMA_VALUE, schema_value))
    89	
    90	    # 2. the five consumer keys, each answered-non-empty.
    91	    for number, key in _CONSUMER_KEYS:
    92	        value = doc.get(key)
    93	        if not _answered_list_of_strings(value):
    94	            problems.append(
    95	                "%s (field %d) must be a non-empty list of non-blank "
    96	                "strings, got %r" % (key, number, value))
    97	
    98	    details = doc.get("details")
    99	    details_is_object = isinstance(details, dict)
   100	    if not details_is_object:
   101	        problems.append("details must be an object, got %r" % (details,))
   102	
   103	    # 3. field 4's law, read from details.notEstablished rather than from
   104	    # the derived top-level whatWasNotEstablished list, because the law is
   105	    # about the SOURCE record, not about whether the derived line happened
   106	    # to be non-empty.
   107	    not_established = details.get("notEstablished") if details_is_object else None
   108	    if not isinstance(not_established, dict):
   109	        problems.append(
   110	            "details.notEstablished must be an object, got %r"
   111	            % (not_established,))
   112	    else:
   113	        items = not_established.get("items")
   114	        justification = not_established.get("noneClaimJustification")
   115	        if not isinstance(items, list):
   116	            problems.append(
   117	                "details.notEstablished.items must be a list, got %r"
   118	                % (items,))
   119	        elif not items:
   120	            if not _answered_string(justification):
   121	                problems.append(
   122	                    "details.notEstablished.items is empty and "
   123	                    "noneClaimJustification is not a non-blank string "
   124	                    "(%r); a literal claim that nothing was left "
   125	                    "unestablished needs its own justification"
   126	                    % (justification,))
   127	        elif not all(_answered_string(v) for v in items):
   128	            problems.append(
   129	                "details.notEstablished.items contains a blank or "
   130	                "non-string entry: %r" % (items,))
   131	
   132	    # 4. change identity: baseCommit / headCommit present and hex-shaped.
   133	    change = doc.get("change")
   134	    if not isinstance(change, dict):
   135	        problems.append("change must be an object, got %r" % (change,))
   136	    else:
   137	        for field in ("baseCommit", "headCommit"):
   138	            value = change.get(field)
   139	            if not _answered_string(value):
   140	                problems.append(
   141	                    "change.%s is required and must be a non-blank "
   142	                    "string, got %r" % (field, value))
   143	            elif not _COMMIT_RE.match(value.strip()):
   144	                problems.append(
   145	                    "change.%s is not hex-shaped (7 to 40 hex characters): "
   146	                    "%r" % (field, value))
   147	
   148	    # 5. accountableHuman: a name, never a role, never blank.
   149	    who = details.get("who") if details_is_object else None
   150	    if not isinstance(who, dict):
   151	        problems.append(
   152	            "details.who must be an object, got %r" % (who,))
   153	    else:
   154	        accountable = who.get("accountableHuman")
   155	        if not _answered_string(accountable):
   156	            problems.append(
   157	                "details.who.accountableHuman is required and must be a "
   158	                "non-blank name, got %r" % (accountable,))
   159	
   160	    # 6. every evidence entry carries origin in {local, ci} and a timestamp.
   161	    evidence = details.get("evidence") if details_is_object else None
   162	    if not isinstance(evidence, list):
   163	        problems.append(
   164	            "details.evidence must be a list, got %r" % (evidence,))
   165	    else:
   166	        for index, entry in enumerate(evidence):
   167	            if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   168	                problems.append(
   169	                    "details.evidence[%d] is not an object: %r"
   170	                    % (index, entry))
   171	                continue
   172	            origin = entry.get("origin")
   173	            if origin not in ("local", "ci"):
   174	                problems.append(
   175	                    "details.evidence[%d].origin must be \"local\" or "
   176	                    "\"ci\", got %r" % (index, origin))
   177	            if not _answered_string(entry.get("timestamp")):
   178	                problems.append(
   179	                    "details.evidence[%d].timestamp is required and must "
   180	                    "be a non-blank string, got %r"
   181	                    % (index, entry.get("timestamp")))
   182	
   183	    return problems
   184	
   185	
   186	def _load(path):
   187	    """Return (doc, error_line_or_None). Never raises: every failure to
   188	    read or parse becomes a plain-English line the caller prints at exit 2,
   189	    because an unreadable file and an invalid document are different facts
   190	    and only the second one is worth exit 1's "here is what to fix"."""
   191	    try:
   192	        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   193	            text = fh.read()
   194	    except OSError as e:
   195	        return None, "could not read %s (%s: %s)" % (path, type(e).__name__, e)
   196	    try:
   197	        return json.loads(text), None
   198	    except ValueError as e:
   199	        return None, "%s is not valid JSON (%s)" % (path, e)
   200	
   201	
   202	def main(argv):
   203	    if len(argv) != 1:
   204	        sys.stderr.write(
   205	            "usage: python3 tools/bm_passport_validator.py <file>\n")
   206	        return 2
   207	    doc, error = _load(argv[0])
   208	    if error is not None:
   209	        sys.stdout.write(error + "\n")
   210	        return 2
   211	
   212	    problems = validate(doc)
   213	    if problems:
   214	        for line in problems:
   215	            sys.stdout.write(line + "\n")
   216	        return 1
   217	    sys.stdout.write("VALID\n")
   218	    return 0
   219	
   220	
   221	if __name__ == "__main__":
   222	    try:
   223	        sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))
   224	    except Exception as exc:
   225	        sys.stdout.write("%s: %s\n" % (type(exc).__name__, exc))
   226	        sys.exit(2)

Unknown top-level keys are ignored by the consumer, so a producer may add its own detail without breaking the contract; this schema still names the ones this producer actually writes.",

     1	# The change passport
     2	
     3	The change passport is the one object that travels from BrotherMode
     4	(execution provenance) to BrotherSBE (assurance). It never travels back. If
     5	the assurance side needs something the passport does not carry, that is a
     6	defect in the passport, not permission to reach into execution state. Full
     7	spec authority: `docs/NORTH-STAR-CHAIN.md`, section "The change passport, in
     8	full".
     9	
    10	This page is the plain reading: what the five fields mean, the shape a
    11	producer actually writes, how to generate one, how to validate one on its
    12	own, and what this first version deliberately does not establish.
    13	
    14	## The five fields
    15	
    16	1. WHAT WAS DONE. The change identity, the commit range, the files touched.
    17	2. WHO DID IT. Which sessions wrote, which claims they held, and the human
    18	   accountable for the result, by name. Accountability is a name, never a
    19	   role.
    20	3. WHAT WAS RUN. Every verification executed after the last edit, its
    21	   result, and whether a build system or a laptop produced it.
    22	4. WHAT WAS NOT ESTABLISHED. The executing side's own honest list of what it
    23	   did not check. MANDATORY, never empty. Regression, performance,
    24	   cross-device, interface behaviour and translated copy belong here by
    25	   default unless something actually examined them.
    26	5. WHERE IT CAME FROM. The development method used, named and not judged.
    27	
    28	## The worked shape
    29	
    30	A passport is one JSON document. The five fields above sit at the top level
    31	as plain-language string lists, so a reader who only wants the fast version
    32	can read those five keys and stop:
    33	
    34	```json
    35	{
    36	  "schema": "change-passport/v1",
    37	  "generatedAt": "2026-08-20T10:00:00Z",
    38	  "whatWasDone": [
    39	    "change in <repo>, commit range <base>..<head>",
    40	    "1 file(s) touched: a.txt"
    41	  ],
    42	  "whoDidIt": [
    43	    "accountable: Fixture Person",
    44	    "session sess-xyz: claims Do the thing, upsert_project"
    45	  ],
    46	  "whatWasRun": [
    47	    "`python3 tools/test_all.py` returned green (local, 2026-08-20T09:30:00Z)"
    48	  ],
    49	  "whatWasNotEstablished": [
    50	    "no regression pass is recorded in the store for this change",
    51	    "..."
    52	  ],
    53	  "whereItCameFrom": [
    54	    "no method plugin detected; native flow"
    55	  ],
    56	  "change": { "repo": "...", "baseCommit": "...", "headCommit": "...", "filesTouched": ["a.txt"] },
    57	  "details": { "who": {...}, "evidence": [...], "method": {...}, "notEstablished": {...} }
    58	}
    59	```
    60	
    61	`change` and `details` are where the five top level fields come from: `change`
    62	holds the commit identity, `details` holds the session, evidence, method and
    63	gap records the five fields are derived from. The full, formal shape is
    64	`schema/change-passport.v1.json`, a JSON Schema draft 2020-12 document any
    65	third party can read without installing anything from this repository.
    66	
    67	## Generating one
    68	
    69	```
    70	python3 tools/bm_passport.py generate --project-id ID --base SHA --head SHA \
    71	    [--accountable NAME] [--method NAME] [--now ISO8601] [--out PATH]
    72	```
    73	
    74	Prints the passport to stdout by default, or writes it to `--out` when
    75	given. `--project-id`, `--base` and `--head` are required. The generator
    76	reads only public record surfaces: `tools/bm_store.py`'s own read accessors
    77	(`get_project`, `list_tasks`, `list_evidence`, `list_attribution`,
    78	`open_key_decisions`), plain `git` for the commit range and the files
    79	touched, and the accountable and method flags. It never reads raw SQLite and
    80	never reads the sentinel surface, which is private by design.
    81	
    82	Exit 0 on a written passport, whatever it could and could not establish.
    83	Exit 1 only when no accountable human could be established at all (no
    84	`--accountable`, nothing in the store, and `git config user.name` empty), or
    85	when `--out` could not be written. Exit 2 for a usage error or a project
    86	root that could not be resolved.
    87	
    88	DETERMINISM. Two generate calls against the same tree with the same `--now`
    89	produce byte identical output. Without `--now`, only `generatedAt` differs.
    90	Everything else, the sorted file list, the sorted session claims, the gap
    91	lines, is assembled in a fixed order rather than left to depend on
    92	iteration order.
    93	
    94	## Validating one, standalone
    95	
    96	```
    97	python3 tools/bm_passport_validator.py <file>
    98	```
    99	
   100	No dependency on this repository beyond the standard library, so a reader
   101	who never installed BrotherMode can still check a passport by hand. Prints
   102	`VALID` and exits 0 when every rule holds. Prints one named reason per line
   103	and exits 1 when the document is well formed JSON but breaks a rule. Exits 2
   104	with a named error when the file cannot be read or is not valid JSON.
   105	
   106	The rules: the `schema` key equals `change-passport/v1`; the five fields
   107	above are each present and answered, meaning a non-empty list of non-blank
   108	strings; field 4's own record (`details.notEstablished`) carries a non-empty
   109	`items` list, or an empty list paired with a non-blank
   110	`noneClaimJustification`, because a literal claim that nothing was left
   111	unestablished needs its own justification; `change.baseCommit` and
   112	`change.headCommit` are present and hex shaped; `details.who.accountableHuman`
   113	is a non-blank name; every entry in `details.evidence` carries an `origin` of
   114	`local` or `ci` and a `timestamp`.
   115	
   116	`schema/fixtures/` carries one valid document
   117	(`change-passport.v1.canonical.json`) and three invalid ones, each breaking
   118	exactly one rule by name: `invalid-hollow-field4.json`,
   119	`invalid-missing-accountable.json`, `invalid-evidence-no-origin.json`.
   120	
   121	## What v1 does not establish
   122	
   123	The fourth field is the honest list this producer could assemble from its
   124	own store and from git alone. Two limits sit above that list, worth stating
   125	once here rather than leaving a reader to discover them:
   126	
   127	Evidence origin. `details.evidence[].origin` is always `local` today,
   128	because BrotherMode's own evidence table records a check's command and note
   129	but not whether a build system ran it. Every entry generated by this tool
   130	carries a line in field 4 saying so, rather than guessing `ci` for anything.
   131	
   132	Trust of `ciRunId` on the consuming side is disclosure level only. BrotherSBE's
   133	own passport reader, `tools/sbe_passport.py`, states this about the value
   134	plainly in its own docstring: a truthy `ciRunId` means the receipt CLAIMS a
   135	build system produced it, never that one actually did, because
   136	`SBE_CI_RUN_ID` is an environment variable any local shell can export. A
   137	forged id reaches the same reading a real one would. This passport format
   138	inherits that same limit wherever a `ciRunId` appears; it is a claim, not
   139	proof.
   140	
   141	Not production ready beyond what the tests in `tools/test_bm_passport.py`
   142	actually exercise: a fixture store built through the same read accessors the
   143	generator uses, and the round trip against BrotherSBE's own consumer where
   144	that sibling repository is present on the machine running the suite.

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Regression tests for tools/bm_passport.py, the change passport's producer
     3	half (docs/specs/2026-08-15-change-passport-seam.md in the BrotherSBE
     4	repository is the contract this tool fills a hole in).
     5	
     6	WHAT THIS SUITE IS ACTUALLY DEFENDING
     7	  Three rules from the contract, each load bearing and each proven by a test
     8	  whose guard was removed by hand to watch it go red:
     9	
    10	  1. A hollow value is never written. An empty string, an empty list, or a
    11	     null reads as absence on the consuming side, so a field this tool
    12	     cannot establish honestly must be OMITTED, never padded to look
    13	     filled.
    14	  2. Direction of travel: this tool writes the deposit and reads NOTHING
    15	     under .sbe/. A poisoned tasks.json sitting next to a healthy store
    16	     must change nothing about what this tool produces.
    17	  3. Absent, corrupt, and healthy stores are three DIFFERENT reported
    18	     states, and none of the three ever crashes the tool.
    19	
    20	Every fixture is a tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(), never the real project
    21	store. The CLI is invoked as a real subprocess so its argument parsing,
    22	exit codes, and stdout are exercised exactly as a caller would see them.
    23	
    24	ADDED: the `generate` verb (schema/change-passport.v1.json), a second,
    25	additive CLI shape on the same tool that writes the full five field
    26	passport rather than the three field deposit above. Its own section below
    27	covers: a round trip from a fixture store built through the Store write
    28	API (never a hand written store file), determinism across two generate
    29	calls sharing one --now, the standalone validator (tools/
    30	bm_passport_validator.py) against the canonical fixture and the three
    31	fixtures that each break exactly one rule, and a read only proof that
    32	BrotherSBE's own consumer (tools/sbe_passport.py in that sibling
    33	repository) reports fields 2 and 5 as CARRIED from a deposited canonical
    34	passport. That last test is guarded: it skips, by name, when the sibling
    35	repository is not present on the machine running this suite.

    37	Standard library only. Run: python3 tools/test_bm_passport.py

    57	    "evidence with no origin": os.path.join(FIXTURES_DIR, "invalid-evidence-no-origin.json"),
    58	}
    59	#: The sibling repository this project's own docs point at throughout

    61	#: the contract proof test below never writes anything under this path.
    62	BROTHERSBE_ROOT = os.path.expanduser("~/Documents/BrotherSBE")

    65	def run_cli(*args, **kw):
    66	    """Invoke the CLI as a real subprocess, with BROTHERMODE_ROOT scrubbed
    67	    so a variable set on the developer's own machine can never leak into a
    68	    test that expects an explicit --root to decide the outcome. `env_over`
    69	    (a dict) is merged on top when a test needs to simulate an environment
    70	    where `git config user.name` has nothing to say."""
    71	    env = dict(os.environ)
    72	    env.pop("BROTHERMODE_ROOT", None)
    73	    env.update(kw.get("env_over") or {})
    74	    return subprocess.run(
    75	        [sys.executable, TOOL_PATH] + list(args),
    76	        stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
    77	        universal_newlines=True, cwd=ROOT, env=env)
    78	
    79	
    80	def write_text(directory, relpath, content="placeholder\n"):
    81	    path = os.path.join(directory, relpath)
    82	    parent = os.path.dirname(path)
    83	    if parent and not os.path.isdir(parent):
    84	        os.makedirs(parent)
    85	    with io.open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
    86	        handle.write(content)
    87	    return path
    88	
    89	
    90	def read_deposit(root, out=None):
    91	    path = out or os.path.join(root, ".sbe", "passport.json")
    92	    with io.open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
    93	        return json.load(handle)
    94	
    95	
    96	def _load_bm_store_module():
    97	    """Load bm_store.py the same way bm_passport.py does, so a test fixture
    98	    can build a real store without shelling out to a second CLI."""
    99	    import importlib.util
   100	    path = os.path.join(HERE, "bm_store.py")
   101	    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
   102	        "bm_store_for_passport_test", path)
   103	    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
   104	    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
   105	    return mod
   106	
   107	
   108	def claim_one(root, session_id="sess-abc", files=("a.py", "b.py")):
   109	    """Build a real, healthy store at `root` carrying one active claim.
   110	    Uses the writable Store directly (not the CLI): this is fixture setup,
   111	    not the thing under test."""
   112	    mod = _load_bm_store_module()
   113	    store = mod.Store(root, create=True)
   114	    try:
   115	        store.claim("unit-a", "ephemeral", objective="test fixture",
   116	                    files=list(files), owner="builder", session_id=session_id)
   117	    finally:
   118	        store.close()
   119	
   120	
   121	def claim_then_park(root, name, session_id, files):
   122	    """Add one claim and move it OUT of the active state, which is the state
   123	    a session's own record is in at the moment it closes and deposits. Parking
   124	    rather than completing on purpose: completing requires evidence, and this
   125	    fixture is about the STATE the producer reads, not about evidence."""
   126	    mod = _load_bm_store_module()
   127	    store = mod.Store(root, create=True)
   128	    try:
   129	        record = store.claim(name, "ephemeral", objective="test fixture",
   130	                             files=list(files), owner="builder",
   131	                             session_id=session_id)
   132	        # Record is a __slots__ object, not a dict: attribute access only.
   133	        # session_id must be passed back: the store's ownership guard refuses
   134	        # a cross-session move of an ACTIVE record, which is the single-writer
   135	        # rule doing its job, so the fixture parks as the same session.
   136	        store.transition(record.lifecycle_uuid, record.version, "parked",
   137	                         session_id=session_id)
   138	    finally:
   139	        store.close()
   140	
   141	
   142	#: An env override that makes `git config user.name` resolve to nothing,
   143	#: without touching this worktree's own git configuration: HOME points at
   144	#: an empty directory (no ~/.gitconfig) and the global/system config files
   145	#: are pointed at /dev/null, so only a repo-local .git/config (which these
   146	#: bare tempdir fixtures never have) could still answer. Applied to the
   147	#: SUBPROCESS environment only, via subprocess.run's own `env=`, never to
   148	#: this test runner's own environment or to any git command this file
   149	#: itself might run.
   150	def _no_git_identity_env(tmp):
   151	    empty_home = os.path.join(tmp, "empty-home")
   152	    os.makedirs(empty_home, exist_ok=True)
   153	    return {
   154	        "HOME": empty_home,
   155	        "GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL": os.devnull,
   156	        "GIT_CONFIG_SYSTEM": os.devnull,
   157	    }
   158

   160	class HelpAndUsageTests(unittest.TestCase):
   161	    def test_help_prints_usage_and_exits_zero(self):
   162	        result = run_cli("--help")
   163	        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   164	        self.assertIn("Usage: ", result.stdout)
   165	        self.assertIn("bm_passport.py", result.stdout)
   166	        self.assertIn("--accountable", result.stdout)
   167	
   168	    def test_usage_never_names_the_repo_relative_path(self):
   169	        """P17: a packaged install has no tools/ directory, so a usage line
   170	        that says `python3 tools/bm_passport.py` names a file the reader
   171	        does not have. This is the local half of the sweep
   172	        test_bm_store.py's TestP17InstructionTextMatchesTheInstalledLayout
   173	        runs over every shipping tool; kept here too so the failure lands
   174	        in this tool's own suite rather than only in the store's."""
   175	        result = run_cli("--help")
   176	        self.assertNotIn("python3 tools/bm_", result.stdout)
   177	        bogus = run_cli("--bogus")
   178	        self.assertNotIn("python3 tools/bm_", bogus.stderr)
   179	
   180	    def test_unknown_argument_is_a_usage_error(self):
   181	        result = run_cli("--bogus")
   182	        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   183	        self.assertIn("unknown argument", result.stderr)
