   186	class ThreeStoreStatesTests(unittest.TestCase):
   187	    """Rule 3: absent, corrupt, and healthy are three DIFFERENT reported
   188	    states, and none of them crashes (exit 0, never a traceback, never
   189	    exit 2's NO-DATA path, since a store being absent is an ordinary,
   190	    fully-expected outcome for this tool, not a failure to determine
   191	    anything)."""
   192	
   193	    def test_absent_store_is_reported_and_does_not_crash(self):
   194	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   195	            result = run_cli("--root", tmp, "--accountable", "Test Person")
   196	            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   197	            self.assertIn("store: absent", result.stdout)
   198	            deposit = read_deposit(tmp)
   199	        self.assertIn("no BrotherMode store",
   200	                      " ".join(deposit["whatWasNotEstablished"]))
   201	
   202	    def test_corrupt_store_is_reported_distinctly_and_does_not_crash(self):
   203	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   204	            write_text(tmp, os.path.join(".brothermode", "store.sqlite3"),
   205	                      "not a real sqlite file" * 5)
   206	            result = run_cli("--root", tmp, "--accountable", "Test Person")
   207	            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   208	            self.assertIn("store: unreadable", result.stdout)
   209	            deposit = read_deposit(tmp)
   210	        joined = " ".join(deposit["whatWasNotEstablished"])
   211	        self.assertIn("could not be", joined)
   212	        self.assertNotIn("no BrotherMode store at", joined)
   213	
   214	    def test_healthy_store_is_reported_as_ok_and_does_not_crash(self):
   215	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   216	            claim_one(tmp, session_id="sess-abc", files=("a.py", "b.py"))
   217	            result = run_cli("--root", tmp, "--accountable", "Test Person")
   218	            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   219	            self.assertIn("store: ok", result.stdout)
   220	            deposit = read_deposit(tmp)

   225	    def test_the_three_states_are_pairwise_distinct(self):
   226	        """Same assertion, phrased the other way: the three states must
   227	        never collapse into each other. Guards against a rewrite that
   228	        makes 'absent' and 'unreadable' report the identical sentence."""
   229	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp_absent, \
   230	             tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp_corrupt, \
   231	             tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp_healthy:
   232	            write_text(tmp_corrupt, os.path.join(".brothermode", "store.sqlite3"),
   233	                      "not a real sqlite file" * 5)
   234	            claim_one(tmp_healthy)
   235	
   236	            r_absent = run_cli("--root", tmp_absent, "--accountable", "P")
   237	            r_corrupt = run_cli("--root", tmp_corrupt, "--accountable", "P")
   238	            r_healthy = run_cli("--root", tmp_healthy, "--accountable", "P")
   239	
   240	        lines = [l for r in (r_absent, r_corrupt, r_healthy)
   241	                for l in r.stdout.splitlines() if l.startswith("store: ")]
   242	        self.assertEqual(len(lines), 3)
   243	        # The STATE WORD, not the whole line. Each line embeds its own
   244	        # tempdir path, so comparing whole lines gave three distinct strings
   245	        # even when the state words collapsed to one: the test passed for the
   246	        # wrong reason until an adversarial review replayed it with all three
   247	        # states set to "unreadable" and watched it stay green.
   248	        states = [l.split("store: ", 1)[1].split()[0] for l in lines]
   249	        self.assertEqual(len(set(states)), 3, states)
   250	        self.assertEqual(states, ["absent", "unreadable", "ok"], states)

   253	class HollowValueTests(unittest.TestCase):
   254	    """Rule 1: an empty string, an empty list, or a null is never written.
   255	    A field this tool cannot establish is OMITTED from the JSON entirely,
   256	    for each of the three fields it owns."""
   257	
   258	    def test_who_did_it_is_omitted_not_written_hollow_when_nothing_carries_it(self):
   259	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   260	            # No store (so no session/claim lines) and no accountable
   261	            # source (no --accountable, and git config is made to answer
   262	            # nothing): whoDidIt has literally nothing to carry.
   263	            result = run_cli("--root", tmp, env_over=_no_git_identity_env(tmp))
   264	            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   265	            deposit = read_deposit(tmp)
   266	        self.assertNotIn("whoDidIt", deposit)
   267	        self.assertIn("whoDidIt: NOT established, omitted", result.stdout)
   268

   270	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   271	            # A whitespace-only --method must not become a hollow deposited
   272	            # string; the tool falls back to its honest default instead of
   273	            # writing "   ".
   274	            result = run_cli("--root", tmp, "--accountable", "Test Person",
   275	                             "--method", "   ")
   276	            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   277	            deposit = read_deposit(tmp)
   278	        self.assertIn("whereItCameFrom", deposit)
   279	        self.assertEqual(deposit["whereItCameFrom"].strip(), deposit["whereItCameFrom"])
   280	        self.assertNotEqual(deposit["whereItCameFrom"].strip(), "")
   281	
   282	    def test_what_was_not_established_is_never_written_as_an_empty_list(self):
   283	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   284	            # The most favorable case this tool can hit: a healthy store
   285	            # with one attributed claim and an explicit accountable name.
   286	            # Even here, whatWasNotEstablished must carry at least the
   287	            # tool's own always-true scope-limit line, never [].
   288	            claim_one(tmp)
   289	            result = run_cli("--root", tmp, "--accountable", "Test Person")
   290	            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   291	            deposit = read_deposit(tmp)

   293	        self.assertTrue(len(deposit["whatWasNotEstablished"]) > 0)
   294	
   295	    def test_no_field_in_the_deposit_is_ever_an_empty_or_null_value(self):
   296	        """Belt and suspenders across ALL THREE store states, not one.
   297	
   298	        This docstring used to claim "every state this tool can hit" while the
   299	        body exercised exactly one (a bare root with no git identity). An
   300	        adversarial review caught the gap: a claim of breadth with a body of
   301	        one is the same overclaim this tool exists to report, in the suite
   302	        that polices it."""
   303	        for label, build in (
   304	                ("absent", lambda d: None),
   305	                ("corrupt", lambda d: write_text(
   306	                    d, os.path.join(".brothermode", "store.sqlite3"),
   307	                    "not a real sqlite file" * 5)),
   308	                ("healthy", lambda d: claim_one(d))):
   309	            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   310	                build(tmp)
   311	                result = run_cli("--root", tmp,
   312	                                 env_over=_no_git_identity_env(tmp))
   313	                self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0,
   314	                                 "%s: %s%s" % (label, result.stdout,
   315	                                               result.stderr))
   316	                deposit = read_deposit(tmp)
   317	            for key, value in deposit.items():
   318	                self.assertNotEqual(value, "", "%s: %s is an empty string"
   319	                                    % (label, key))
   320	                self.assertNotEqual(value, [], "%s: %s is an empty list"
   321	                                    % (label, key))
   322	                self.assertIsNotNone(value, "%s: %s is null" % (label, key))
   323	
   324	    def test_the_single_state_version_of_the_check_still_holds(self):
   325	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   326	            result = run_cli("--root", tmp, env_over=_no_git_identity_env(tmp))
   327	            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   328	            deposit = read_deposit(tmp)
   329	        for key, value in deposit.items():
   330	            self.assertTrue(value is not None, key)
   331	            if isinstance(value, str):
   332	                self.assertNotEqual(value.strip(), "", key)
   333	            if isinstance(value, list):
   334	                self.assertGreater(len(value), 0, key)

   337	class DirectionOfTravelTests(unittest.TestCase):
   338	    """Rule 2: this tool writes the deposit and reads NOTHING under .sbe/.
   339	    A poisoned tasks.json sitting next to the exact same store must not
   340	    change one byte of what this tool produces."""
   341	
   342	    def test_a_poisoned_sbe_tasks_json_changes_nothing_about_the_output(self):
   343	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   344	            claim_one(tmp, session_id="sess-fixed", files=("x.py",))
   345	
   346	            before = run_cli("--root", tmp, "--out",
   347	                             os.path.join(tmp, "before.json"),
   348	                             "--accountable", "Test Person")
   349	            self.assertEqual(before.returncode, 0, before.stdout + before.stderr)
   350	            deposit_before = read_deposit(tmp, out=os.path.join(tmp, "before.json"))
   351	
   352	            # Poisoned on purpose: invalid JSON, so ANY read of it (even a
   353	            # failed one that leaked into the output) would show up as a
   354	            # difference from the "before" run.
   355	            write_text(tmp, os.path.join(".sbe", "tasks.json"),
   356	                      "{ this is not valid json at all !!! ")
   357	            write_text(tmp, os.path.join(".sbe", "evidence", "fake.json"),
   358	                      '{"command": "rm -rf /", "verdict": "PASS"}')
   359	            write_text(tmp, os.path.join(".sbe", "passport.json"),
   360	                      '{"whoDidIt": ["a lie planted by the assurance side"]}')
   361	
   362	            after = run_cli("--root", tmp, "--out",
   363	                            os.path.join(tmp, "after.json"),
   364	                            "--accountable", "Test Person")
   365	            self.assertEqual(after.returncode, 0, after.stdout + after.stderr)
   366	            deposit_after = read_deposit(tmp, out=os.path.join(tmp, "after.json"))
   367	
   368	        self.assertEqual(deposit_before, deposit_after)
   369	        self.assertNotIn("a lie planted by the assurance side",
   370	                         json.dumps(deposit_after))

   373	class DepositShapeTests(unittest.TestCase):
   374	    """The deposit keys match the contract's deposit key column exactly,

   376	    merged or dropped."""
   377	
   378	    def test_deposit_uses_the_exact_contract_keys(self):
   379	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:

   386	                                "whatWasNotEstablished"))

   388	        # check: an adversarial review replayed the loop above against {} and
   389	        # against a deposit missing whoDidIt entirely, and it passed both, so
   390	        # a regression that dropped a field would have stayed green.
   391	        for key in ("whoDidIt", "whereItCameFrom", "whatWasNotEstablished"):
   392	            self.assertIn(key, deposit, deposit)

   417	class SilentGapTests(unittest.TestCase):
   418	    """The regression suite for the defect an adversarial review found: a
   419	    CONFIDENTLY WRONG field 2 beside a field 4 that says nothing is missing.
   420	
   421	    Field 4 is the product's whole claim, the one thing no green gate can
   422	    fake, so every way this producer can be partially blind gets a test that
   423	    fails if the disclosure is removed. Each test asserts the SPECIFIC
   424	    sentence, not merely that field 4 is non-empty: an unconditional scope
   425	    line is always present, so 'field 4 has content' would pass with every
   426	    real gap silently dropped, which is exactly how the defect survived
   427	    fourteen passing tests."""
   428	
   429	    def test_a_closed_record_is_named_rather_than_silently_skipped(self):
   430	        """THE CRITICAL. Only active records are read, and a session deposits
   431	        at CLOSE, when its own record is most likely already parked or
   432	        complete. So the tool could name a concurrent, unrelated session as
   433	        the author and report no gap at all."""
   434	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   435	            claim_then_park(tmp, "did-the-work", "sess-DID-THE-WORK",
   436	                            ["x.py", "y.py"])
   437	            claim_one(tmp, session_id="sess-OTHER-CONCURRENT",
   438	                      files=("unrelated.py",))
   439	            result = run_cli("--root", tmp, "--accountable", "Test Person")
   440	            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   441	            deposit = read_deposit(tmp)
   442	
   443	        gaps = " ".join(deposit.get("whatWasNotEstablished", []))
   444	        self.assertIn("not active", gaps, deposit)
   445	        self.assertIn("were NOT read", gaps, deposit)
   446	        # The other session is still named, which is honest ONLY because the
   447	        # gap above tells the reader they are seeing active claims alone.
   448	        self.assertIn("sess-OTHER-CONCURRENT",
   449	                      " ".join(deposit.get("whoDidIt", [])), deposit)
   450	
   451	    def test_a_git_derived_name_says_it_was_inferred(self):
   452	        """`git config user.name` answers who owns the laptop, not who is
   453	        accountable for this change, and answers even outside a repository."""
   454	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   455	            claim_one(tmp)
   456	            result = run_cli("--root", tmp)   # no --accountable on purpose
   457	            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   458	            deposit = read_deposit(tmp)
   459	
   460	        gaps = " ".join(deposit.get("whatWasNotEstablished", []))
   461	        who = " ".join(deposit.get("whoDidIt", []))
   462	        if "accountable:" in who:
   463	            self.assertIn("INFERRED", gaps, deposit)
   464	            self.assertIn("git config", gaps, deposit)
   465	        else:
   466	            # No git identity reachable: the other honest answer, and the
   467	            # field must then be omitted rather than padded.
   468	            self.assertIn("could not be established", gaps, deposit)
   469	
   470	    def test_an_explicit_accountable_name_carries_no_inference_gap(self):
   471	        """The control for the test above. Without it, asserting the INFERRED
   472	        sentence would still pass if the tool emitted that sentence always."""
   473	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   474	            claim_one(tmp)
   475	            result = run_cli("--root", tmp, "--accountable", "Stated Person")
   476	            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   477	            deposit = read_deposit(tmp)
   478	
   479	        self.assertNotIn("INFERRED",
   480	                         " ".join(deposit.get("whatWasNotEstablished", [])))
   481	        self.assertIn("accountable: Stated Person",
   482	                      " ".join(deposit.get("whoDidIt", [])))
   483	
   484	    def test_a_session_claiming_no_files_is_named_as_a_gap(self):
   485	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   486	            claim_one(tmp, session_id="sess-nofiles", files=())
   487	            result = run_cli("--root", tmp, "--accountable", "Test Person")
   488	            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   489	            deposit = read_deposit(tmp)
   490	
   491	        self.assertIn("claim no files at all",
   492	                      " ".join(deposit.get("whatWasNotEstablished", [])), deposit)

   495	class PathFlagTests(unittest.TestCase):
   496	    """An empty value for a flag that decides WHERE bytes land is refused,
   497	    never treated as absent. `--out "$OUT"` with an unset variable used to
   498	    fall through to the project's real .sbe/passport.json."""
   499	
   500	    def test_empty_out_is_refused_rather_than_writing_the_default(self):
   501	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   502	            claim_one(tmp)
   503	            result = run_cli("--root", tmp, "--out", "", "--accountable", "P")
   504	            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   505	            self.assertIn("empty value", result.stderr)
   506	            self.assertFalse(
   507	                os.path.isfile(os.path.join(tmp, ".sbe", "passport.json")),
   508	                "an empty --out silently wrote the default deposit path")
   509	
   510	    def test_empty_root_is_refused(self):
   511	        result = run_cli("--root", "", "--accountable", "P")
   512	        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   513	        self.assertIn("empty value", result.stderr)
   514	
   515	    def test_an_unresolvable_root_is_exit_2_and_says_NO_DATA(self):
   516	        """The exit 2 path, which the docstring documents and nothing asserted.
   517	
   518	        Three exits are documented: 0 when a deposit was written whatever it
   519	        could establish, 1 when the deposit could not be written, and 2 for a
   520	        usage error or a root that could not be resolved at all. The last is
   521	        not a finding about a change, it is a failure to find a change to
   522	        report on, and reporting it as 0 would be the tool claiming it looked
   523	        when it did not."""
   524	        missing = os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(),
   525	                               "bm-passport-no-such-dir-xyzzy")
   526	        self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(missing))
   527	        result = run_cli("--root", missing, "--accountable", "P")
   528	        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   529	        self.assertIn("NO-DATA", result.stdout)
   530	
   531	    def test_a_failed_write_leaves_the_previous_deposit_intact(self):
   532	        """What temp-plus-replace actually buys, asserted rather than assumed.
   533	
   534	        Writing the deposit in place truncates it first, so a failure mid-write
   535	        leaves a half-written file that the consumer classifies as CORRUPT,
   536	        which is a worse answer than the previous deposit or than none at all.
   537	        The failure is injected by making the temp path unwritable (a directory
   538	        sits where the temp FILE must go), which is the closest reachable
   539	        analogue of a disk failure without root."""
   540	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   541	            claim_one(tmp)
   542	            out = os.path.join(tmp, "deposit.json")
   543	
   544	            first = run_cli("--root", tmp, "--out", out, "--accountable", "First")
   545	            self.assertEqual(first.returncode, 0, first.stdout + first.stderr)
   546	            with io.open(out, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   547	                before = fh.read()
   548	            self.assertIn("First", before)
   549	
   550	            # A directory where the temp FILE must be written: open() raises
   551	            # IsADirectoryError, an OSError, on the temp path only.
   552	            os.mkdir(out + ".tmp")
   553	
   554	            second = run_cli("--root", tmp, "--out", out,
   555	                             "--accountable", "Second")
   556	            self.assertEqual(second.returncode, 1,
   557	                             second.stdout + second.stderr)
   558	            self.assertIn("could not write", second.stderr)
   559	
   560	            with io.open(out, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   561	                after = fh.read()
   562	        self.assertEqual(before, after,
   563	                         "the failed write changed the previous deposit; "
   564	                         "temp-plus-replace is not protecting it")
   565	        json.loads(after)   # still parseable, so never classified CORRUPT
   566	
   567	    def test_no_temp_file_is_left_behind_by_a_successful_write(self):
   568	        """The deposit is written to a .tmp path and moved into place, so a
   569	        failure mid-write cannot leave a truncated deposit the consumer would
   570	        classify as corrupt. The move must actually happen."""
   571	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   572	            claim_one(tmp)
   573	            out = os.path.join(tmp, "deposit.json")
   574	            result = run_cli("--root", tmp, "--out", out, "--accountable", "P")
   575	            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   576	            self.assertTrue(os.path.isfile(out))
   577	            self.assertFalse(os.path.isfile(out + ".tmp"))

   580	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   581	# `generate`: the full five field passport (schema/change-passport.v1.json).
   582	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   583	
   584	def _git(args, cwd, env=None):
   585	    full_env = dict(os.environ, GIT_AUTHOR_NAME="t", GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL="t@example.com",
   586	                    GIT_COMMITTER_NAME="t", GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL="t@example.com")
   587	    if env:
   588	        full_env.update(env)
   589	    return subprocess.run(["git"] + list(args), cwd=cwd, env=full_env,
   590	                          stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
   591	                          universal_newlines=True, timeout=60)
   592	
   593	
   594	def make_repo(tmp):
   595	    """A two commit repository at `tmp`. Returns (base, head)."""
   596	    _git(["init", "-q"], tmp)
   597	    write_text(tmp, "a.txt", "one\n")
   598	    _git(["add", "-A"], tmp)
   599	    _git(["commit", "-q", "-m", "one"], tmp)
   600	    base = _git(["rev-parse", "HEAD"], tmp).stdout.strip()
   601	    write_text(tmp, "a.txt", "two\n")
   602	    _git(["add", "-A"], tmp)
   603	    _git(["commit", "-q", "-m", "two"], tmp)
   604	    head = _git(["rev-parse", "HEAD"], tmp).stdout.strip()
   605	    return base, head
   606	
   607	
   608	def make_project_fixture(tmp, project_id="proj-1", actor_name="Fixture Person",
   609	                         session_id="sess-xyz", with_evidence=True):
   610	    """A real, healthy store at `tmp` carrying one project, one task, and
   611	    (unless with_evidence is False) one evidence row, all written through
   612	    bm_store.py's own write API, never a hand written store file. Every
   613	    write shares one actor, so the attribution log names one session."""
   614	    mod = _load_bm_store_module()
   615	    store = mod.Store(tmp, create=True)
   616	    actor = {"actor_type": "human", "actor_name": actor_name,
   617	             "session_id": session_id}
   618	    try:
   619	        store.upsert_project({
   620	            "project_id": project_id, "name": "Fixture Project",
   621	            "created_at": "2026-08-20T09:00:00Z",
   622	            "updated_at": "2026-08-20T09:00:00Z",
   623	        }, actor)
   624	        task_id = store.create_task({
   625	            "task_id": "task-1", "project_id": project_id,
   626	            "title": "Do the thing", "status": "planned",
   627	        }, actor)
   628	        if with_evidence:
   629	            store.add_evidence({
   630	                "evidence_id": "ev-1", "subject_type": "task",
   631	                "subject_id": task_id, "kind": "test",
   632	                "ref": "python3 tools/test_all.py", "note": "green",
   633	                "created_at": "2026-08-20T09:30:00Z",
   634	            }, project_id, actor)
   635	    finally:
   636	        store.close()
   637	    return task_id
   638	
   639	
   640	def run_generate(*args, **kw):
   641	    env = dict(os.environ)
   642	    env.pop("BROTHERMODE_ROOT", None)
   643	    env.update(kw.get("env_over") or {})
   644	    return subprocess.run(
   645	        [sys.executable, TOOL_PATH, "generate"] + list(args),
   646	        stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
   647	        universal_newlines=True, cwd=ROOT, env=env)
   648	
   649	
   650	class GenerateRoundTripTests(unittest.TestCase):
   651	    """A fixture store built through the write API, read back by `generate`
   652	    through the five allowed accessors. Structural assertions, not exact
   653	    string equality, on everything that came out of the store: what
   654	    matters here is that a real name and a real command surface, not that
   655	    they land in one particular sentence shape a later refactor might
   656	    reword."""
   657	
   658	    def test_round_trip_carries_real_session_and_evidence_data(self):
   659	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   660	            base, head = make_repo(tmp)
   661	            make_project_fixture(tmp)
   662	            result = run_generate("--root", tmp, "--project-id", "proj-1",
   663	                                  "--base", base, "--head", head,
   664	                                  "--now", "2026-08-20T10:00:00Z")
   665	        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   666	        passport = json.loads(result.stdout)
   667	
   668	        self.assertEqual(passport["schema"], "change-passport/v1")
   669	        self.assertEqual(passport["change"]["baseCommit"], base)
   670	        self.assertEqual(passport["change"]["headCommit"], head)
   671	        self.assertEqual(passport["change"]["filesTouched"], ["a.txt"])
   672	
   673	        who = passport["details"]["who"]
   674	        self.assertEqual(who["accountableHuman"], "Fixture Person")
   675	        self.assertEqual(len(who["sessions"]), 1)
   676	        self.assertEqual(who["sessions"][0]["label"], "sess-xyz")
   677	        self.assertIn("Do the thing", who["sessions"][0]["claims"])
   678	
   679	        evidence = passport["details"]["evidence"]
   680	        self.assertEqual(len(evidence), 1)
   681	        self.assertEqual(evidence[0]["command"], "python3 tools/test_all.py")
   682	        self.assertEqual(evidence[0]["verdict"], "green")
   683	
   684	    def test_no_project_in_the_store_still_produces_a_passport(self):
   685	        """The store exists but the requested project does not: a named
   686	        gap, never a crash and never a refusal (the generator reports, it
   687	        does not block, on anything short of the accountable law)."""
   688	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   689	            base, head = make_repo(tmp)
   690	            make_project_fixture(tmp, project_id="some-other-project")
   691	            result = run_generate("--root", tmp, "--project-id", "missing",
   692	                                  "--base", base, "--head", head,
   693	                                  "--accountable", "Test Person")
   694	        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   695	        passport = json.loads(result.stdout)
   696	        self.assertIn("no project", " ".join(passport["whatWasNotEstablished"]))

   699	class GenerateDeterminismTests(unittest.TestCase):
   700	    def test_two_generates_with_the_same_now_are_byte_identical(self):
   701	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   702	            base, head = make_repo(tmp)
   703	            make_project_fixture(tmp)
   704	            out1 = os.path.join(tmp, "one.json")
   705	            out2 = os.path.join(tmp, "two.json")
   706	            first = run_generate("--root", tmp, "--project-id", "proj-1",
   707	                                 "--base", base, "--head", head,
   708	                                 "--now", "2026-08-20T10:00:00Z", "--out", out1)
   709	            second = run_generate("--root", tmp, "--project-id", "proj-1",
   710	                                  "--base", base, "--head", head,
   711	                                  "--now", "2026-08-20T10:00:00Z", "--out", out2)
   712	            self.assertEqual(first.returncode, 0, first.stdout + first.stderr)
   713	            self.assertEqual(second.returncode, 0, second.stdout + second.stderr)
   714	            with io.open(out1, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   715	                bytes1 = fh.read()
   716	            with io.open(out2, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   717	                bytes2 = fh.read()
   718	        self.assertEqual(bytes1, bytes2,
   719	                         "two generate calls sharing the same --now produced "
   720	                         "different bytes")
   721	
   722	    def test_generatedAt_is_the_only_thing_that_differs_without_now(self):
   723	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   724	            base, head = make_repo(tmp)
   725	            make_project_fixture(tmp)
   726	            first = run_generate("--root", tmp, "--project-id", "proj-1",
   727	                                 "--base", base, "--head", head)
   728	            second = run_generate("--root", tmp, "--project-id", "proj-1",
   729	                                  "--base", base, "--head", head)
   730	        self.assertEqual(first.returncode, 0, first.stdout + first.stderr)
   731	        self.assertEqual(second.returncode, 0, second.stdout + second.stderr)
   732	        p1, p2 = json.loads(first.stdout), json.loads(second.stdout)
   733	        del p1["generatedAt"]
   734	        del p2["generatedAt"]
   735	        self.assertEqual(p1, p2)

   795	class ValidatorTests(unittest.TestCase):
   796	    """The standalone validator (tools/bm_passport_validator.py) against
   797	    the canonical fixture and each of the three fixtures that break
   798	    exactly one rule."""
   799	
   800	    def run_validator(self, path):
   801	        return subprocess.run(
   802	            [sys.executable, VALIDATOR_PATH, path],
   803	            stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
   804	            universal_newlines=True)
   805	
   806	    def test_canonical_fixture_is_valid(self):
   807	        result = self.run_validator(CANONICAL_FIXTURE)
   808	        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   809	        self.assertEqual(result.stdout.strip(), "VALID")
   810	
   811	    def test_hollow_field_4_is_named(self):
   812	        result = self.run_validator(INVALID_FIXTURES["hollow field 4"])
   813	        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 1, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   814	        self.assertIn("notEstablished", result.stdout)
   815	
   816	    def test_missing_accountable_is_named(self):
   817	        result = self.run_validator(INVALID_FIXTURES["missing accountable"])
   818	        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 1, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   819	        self.assertIn("accountableHuman", result.stdout)
   820	
   821	    def test_evidence_with_no_origin_is_named(self):
   822	        result = self.run_validator(INVALID_FIXTURES["evidence with no origin"])
   823	        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 1, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   824	        self.assertIn("origin", result.stdout)
   825	
   826	    def test_unreadable_file_is_a_named_error_at_exit_2(self):
   827	        result = self.run_validator(
   828	            os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(), "bm-passport-no-such-file.json"))
   829	        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   830	        self.assertIn("could not read", result.stdout)
   831	
   832	    def test_non_json_file_is_a_named_error_at_exit_2(self):
   833	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   834	            path = write_text(tmp, "not-json.json", "{ this is not json")
   835	            result = self.run_validator(path)
   836	        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   837	        self.assertIn("not valid JSON", result.stdout)

   840	class BrotherSBEContractProofTests(unittest.TestCase):
   841	    """Read only proof that the canonical fixture, deposited exactly where
   842	    the sibling repository expects it, makes BrotherSBE's own consumer
   843	    report fields 2 and 5 as CARRIED. Never writes into BROTHERSBE_ROOT:
   844	    the fixture store lives in a temp dir, and BrotherSBE's tool is only
   845	    ever invoked with --root pointed at that temp dir."""
   846	
   847	    def setUp(self):
   848	        sbe_passport = os.path.join(BROTHERSBE_ROOT, "tools", "sbe_passport.py")
   849	        if not os.path.isfile(sbe_passport):
   850	            self.skipTest(
   851	                "BrotherSBE is not present at %s on this machine, so the "
   852	                "contract proof cannot run; this is a skip, never a pass, "
   853	                "and never a fail" % sbe_passport)
   854	        self.sbe_passport = sbe_passport
   855	
   856	    def test_fields_two_and_five_are_carried_from_the_deposit(self):
   857	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   858	            sbe_dir = os.path.join(tmp, ".sbe")
   859	            os.makedirs(sbe_dir)
   860	            # Minimal tasks.json, mirroring BrotherSBE's own
   861	            # tools/test_sbe_passport.py fixture shape: an empty task list
   862	            # is a legal, usable registry for this seam, since fields 2 and
   863	            # 5 come from the deposit alone, never from tasks.
   864	            with io.open(os.path.join(sbe_dir, "tasks.json"), "w",
   865	                         encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   866	                json.dump({"tasks": []}, fh)
   867	            with io.open(CANONICAL_FIXTURE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   868	                canonical_bytes = fh.read()
   869	            with io.open(os.path.join(sbe_dir, "passport.json"), "w",
   870	                         encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   871	                fh.write(canonical_bytes)
   872	
   873	            result = subprocess.run(
   874	                [sys.executable, self.sbe_passport, "--root", tmp, "--json"],
   875	                stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
   876	                universal_newlines=True)
   877	        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
   878	        report = json.loads(result.stdout)
   879	        states = {f["number"]: f["state"] for f in report["fields"]}
   880	        self.assertEqual(states.get(2), "CARRIED",
   881	                         "field 2 (who did it) was not reported CARRIED: %s"
   882	                         % report)
   883	        self.assertEqual(states.get(5), "CARRIED",
   884	                         "field 5 (where it came from) was not reported "
   885	                         "CARRIED: %s" % report)
   886	
   887	
   888	if __name__ == "__main__":
   889	    unittest.main()

   383	
   384	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   385	# `generate`: the full five-field passport (schema/change-passport.v1.json).
   386	#
   387	# Reads ONLY the five ReadOnlyStore accessors named in the task brief
   388	# (get_project, list_tasks, list_evidence, list_attribution,
   389	# open_key_decisions), plus git (subprocess, explicit failure paths) and
   390	# the --accountable / --method / --now flags. Never touches the fence
   391	# tables (records, claims) the no-verb deposit above reads through
   392	# store.dump(); "who did it" is rebuilt here from the attribution log
   393	# instead, which is one of the five allowed surfaces.
   394	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   395	
   396	#: Default gap lines, one per keyword. A category is DROPPED only when that
   397	#: keyword (or its space-separated spelling) turns up in a task title or an
   398	#: evidence row's kind/ref/note, i.e. the store shows it was actually
   399	#: examined. This is a heuristic over free text, disclosed as one here and
   400	#: in docs/PASSPORT.md, not a claim of certainty.
   401	_DEFAULT_GAP_KEYWORDS = (
   402	    ("regression", "no regression pass is recorded in the store for this change"),
   403	    ("performance", "no performance measurement is recorded in the store for this change"),
   404	    ("cross-device", "no cross-device check is recorded in the store for this change"),
   405	    ("interface", "no interface behaviour review is recorded in the store for this change"),
   406	    ("translat", "the translated copy was not read by anyone who speaks the "
   407	                 "language, per this record"),
   408	)
   409	
   410	
   411	def _run_git(root, args):
   412	    """(stdout_or_None, error_or_None). Never raises: a missing git binary,
   413	    a bad ref, or a non-repository root all become a plain-English reason
   414	    string, never a traceback and never a silently empty result."""
   415	    try:
   416	        result = subprocess.run(
   417	            ["git", "-C", root] + list(args),
   418	            stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
   419	            text=True, encoding="utf-8", timeout=30)
   420	    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as e:
   421	        return None, "%s: %s" % (type(e).__name__, e)
   422	    if result.returncode != 0:
   423	        return None, (result.stderr.strip() or
   424	                      "git %s exited %d" % (" ".join(args), result.returncode))
   425	    return result.stdout, None
   426	
   427	
   428	def _repo_identity(root):
   429	    """The origin remote URL, or the directory name when there is none.
   430	    Never raises and never returns an empty string: a directory name is
   431	    always available as the last resort."""
   432	    out, _err = _run_git(root, ["remote", "get-url", "origin"])
   433	    if out and out.strip():
   434	        return out.strip()
   435	    return os.path.basename(os.path.abspath(root).rstrip(os.sep)) or root
   436	
   437	
   438	def _files_touched(root, base, head):
   439	    """(sorted_files, gap_reason_or_None). A git failure is reported as a
   440	    NAMED gap and an empty list, never a silent empty list with no
   441	    explanation (the design law this function exists to satisfy)."""
   442	    out, err = _run_git(root, ["diff", "--name-only", "%s..%s" % (base, head)])
   443	    if err is not None:
   444	        return [], ("git diff --name-only %s..%s failed: %s, so the files "
   445	                    "touched by this change could not be established"
   446	                    % (base, head, err))
   447	    files = sorted(set(line.strip() for line in out.splitlines() if line.strip()))
   448	    return files, None
   449	
   450	
   451	def _open_readonly_store(root):
   452	    """(store_or_None, state, reason). state is one of 'ok', 'absent',
   453	    'unreadable'. The caller is responsible for closing a returned store.
   454	    Mirrors _store_snapshot's three-state shape above so both verbs report
   455	    an absent or corrupt store the same honest way, without sharing code
   456	    that would otherwise open the store TWICE for one invocation."""
   457	    mod, err = _load_bm_store()
   458	    if mod is None:
   459	        return None, "unreadable", "bm_store.py could not be loaded (%s)" % err
   460	    store_path = mod.store_path(root)
   461	    try:
   462	        store = mod.ReadOnlyStore(root)
   463	    except mod.OwnershipRefused as e:
   464	        if e.reason == "no-store":
   465	            return None, "absent", "no BrotherMode store at %s" % store_path
   466	        return None, "unreadable", ("the BrotherMode store at %s could not "
   467	                                    "be opened: %s" % (store_path, e))
   468	    except mod.StoreCorrupt as e:
   469	        return None, "unreadable", ("the BrotherMode store at %s is "
   470	                                    "corrupt: %s" % (store_path, e))
   471	    except Exception as e:
   472	        return None, "unreadable", ("the BrotherMode store at %s could not "
   473	                                    "be opened: %s: %s"
   474	                                    % (store_path, type(e).__name__, e))
   475	    return store, "ok", None
   476	
   477	
   478	def _session_lines(attribution_rows, tasks_by_id):
   479	    """Every session named on the attribution log for this project, sorted
   480	    by session id, each with the claims (output artifacts, or else the
   481	    task it touched, or else the action name) it can be shown to hold.
   482	    Returns a list of {"label", "claims"} dicts, schema/change-
   483	    passport.v1.json's own shape for details.who.sessions."""
   484	    groups = {}
   485	    for row in attribution_rows:
   486	        sid = (row.get("session_id") or "").strip()
   487	        if not sid:
   488	            continue
   489	        claims = groups.setdefault(sid, set())
   490	        artifacts = [a for a in (row.get("output_artifacts") or []) if a]
   491	        if artifacts:
   492	            for artifact in artifacts:
   493	                claims.add(str(artifact))
   494	        else:
   495	            task_id = row.get("task_id")
   496	            if task_id and task_id in tasks_by_id:
   497	                claims.add(tasks_by_id[task_id].get("title") or task_id)
   498	            elif row.get("action"):
   499	                claims.add(str(row["action"]))
   500	    return [{"label": sid, "claims": sorted(groups[sid])} for sid in sorted(groups)]
   501	
   502	
   503	def _resolve_accountable(root, explicit, attribution_rows):
   504	    """(name_or_None, gap_reason_or_None). Three sources in order:
   505	    --accountable, the newest human actor on the attribution log (already
   506	    newest-first per list_attribution's own ORDER BY), `git config
   507	    user.name`. None for both means every source came up empty; the
   508	    caller turns that into the hard generation error the design names."""
   509	    if explicit and explicit.strip():
   510	        return explicit.strip(), None
   511	    for row in attribution_rows:
   512	        name = (row.get("actor_name") or "").strip()
   513	        if row.get("actor_type") == "human" and name:
   514	            return name, ("the accountable person was INFERRED from the "
   515	                          "newest human attribution event recorded for this "
   516	                          "project; no --accountable flag was given")
   517	    name = _git_user_name(root)
   518	    if name:
   519	        return name, ("the accountable person was INFERRED from `git "
   520	                      "config user.name` at %s, which resolves the "
   521	                      "machine's global git identity; no --accountable flag "
   522	                      "was given and the store named no human actor" % root)
   523	    return None, None
   524	
   525	
   526	def _method_name(method_flag, decision_rows):
   527	    """(name, was_defaulted). --method wins outright. Otherwise the newest
   528	    open DECISION insight whose decision_class is "method" (open_key_
   529	    decisions is already newest-first) supplies the name the store itself
   530	    recorded. Only the hard-coded default is used when neither exists."""
   531	    if method_flag and method_flag.strip():
   532	        return method_flag.strip(), False
   533	    for row in decision_rows:
   534	        if (row.get("decision_class") or "").strip().lower() == "method":
   535	            candidate = (row.get("subject") or row.get("claim") or "").strip()
   536	            if candidate:
   537	                return candidate, False
   538	    return "no method plugin detected; native flow", True
   539	
   540	
   541	def _evidence_entries(evidence_pairs):
   542	    """[(task_dict, entry_dict), ...] -> details.evidence entries. Origin
   543	    is always "local": the BrotherMode evidence table (kind/ref/note) has
   544	    no column recording whether a build system produced a check, unlike
   545	    BrotherSBE's own receipts (ciRunId). Reported honestly as a named gap
   546	    by the caller rather than guessed per entry."""
   547	    entries = []
   548	    for _task, row in evidence_pairs:
   549	        command = (row.get("ref") or row.get("kind") or "").strip() or "(unspecified check)"
   550	        verdict = (row.get("note") or "").strip() or "(no verdict recorded)"
   551	        entries.append({
   552	            "command": command,
   553	            "verdict": verdict,
   554	            "origin": "local",
   555	            "timestamp": row.get("created_at") or "",
   556	        })
   557	    return entries
   558	
   559	
   560	def _examined_text_blob(tasks, evidence_pairs):
   561	    parts = [str(t.get("title") or "") for t in tasks]
   562	    for _task, row in evidence_pairs:
   563	        parts.append(str(row.get("kind") or ""))
   564	        parts.append(str(row.get("ref") or ""))
   565	        parts.append(str(row.get("note") or ""))
   566	    return " ".join(parts).lower()
   567	
   568	
   569	def _default_gap_lines(tasks, evidence_pairs):
   570	    blob = _examined_text_blob(tasks, evidence_pairs)
   571	    lines = []
   572	    for keyword, sentence in _DEFAULT_GAP_KEYWORDS:
   573	        if keyword in blob or keyword.replace("-", " ") in blob:
   574	            continue
   575	        lines.append(sentence)
   576	    return lines
   577	
   578	
   579	def _canonical_now(now_arg):
   580	    """(iso_string_or_None, error_or_None). Canonicalises to seconds-
   581	    precision UTC ("...Z") whether the caller passed --now or not, so two
   582	    invocations with the SAME --now produce byte-identical output
   583	    regardless of the offset form given (a trailing Z, a numeric offset,
   584	    or none at all)."""
   585	    if now_arg:
   586	        text = now_arg
   587	        if text.endswith("Z"):
   588	            text = text[:-1] + "+00:00"
   589	        try:
   590	            parsed = datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(text)
   591	        except ValueError as e:
   592	            return None, "invalid --now value %r: %s" % (now_arg, e)
   593	        if parsed.tzinfo is None:
   594	            parsed = parsed.replace(tzinfo=datetime.timezone.utc)
   595	        parsed = parsed.astimezone(datetime.timezone.utc)
   596	    else:
   597	        parsed = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc)
   598	    return parsed.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"), None
   599	
   600	
   601	def build_passport(root, project_id, base, head, accountable_flag, method_flag,
   602	                   now_arg):
   603	    """(passport_dict_or_None, error_or_None). error set means generation
   604	    refused outright (only the empty-accountable law does this); every
   605	    other gap is folded into details.notEstablished / whatWasNotEstablished
   606	    instead, per the design's "reports, does not block" rule."""
   607	    now_str, now_err = _canonical_now(now_arg)
   608	    if now_err:
   609	        return None, now_err
   610	
   611	    repo = _repo_identity(root)
   612	    files, files_gap = _files_touched(root, base, head)
   613	
   614	    store, state, reason = _open_readonly_store(root)
   615	    tasks, attribution, decisions, evidence_pairs = [], [], [], []
   616	    store_gap = None
   617	    if state == "ok":
   618	        try:
   619	            # raw=True on every one of the five allowed accessors, on
   620	            # purpose: their default (raw=False) applies bm_store.py's own
   621	            # founder-text scrubber (_export_row), which withholds free text
   622	            # as "[WITHHELD: N chars of founder text]". That is the right
   623	            # default for an untrusted dump, but a passport's whole job is
   624	            # to carry REAL session labels, task titles and check commands;
   625	            # a redacted placeholder in whoDidIt or whatWasRun is not a
   626	            # privacy win, it is a passport that lies by looking filled.
   627	            # Precedent: the no-verb deposit path above already reads
   628	            # store.dump(raw=True) for the identical reason, and raw=True
   629	            # opens "no new disclosure surface beyond what dump(raw=True)
   630	            # already grants" per _export_row's own docstring.
   631	            project = store.get_project(project_id, raw=True)
   632	            tasks = store.list_tasks(project_id, raw=True)
   633	            attribution = store.list_attribution(project_id, limit=500, raw=True)
   634	            decisions = store.open_key_decisions(project_id, raw=True)
   635	            for task in tasks:
   636	                for row in store.list_evidence("task", task["task_id"], raw=True):
   637	                    evidence_pairs.append((task, row))
   638	            if project is None:
   639	                store_gap = ("no project %r was found in the BrotherMode "
   640	                             "store, so its tasks, evidence and decisions "
   641	                             "could not be read" % project_id)
   642	        finally:
   643	            store.close()
   644	    else:
   645	        store_gap = "%s, so session, evidence and decision history for this change could not be read" % reason
   646	
   647	    tasks_by_id = {t["task_id"]: t for t in tasks}
   648	    sessions = _session_lines(attribution, tasks_by_id)
   649	    accountable_name, accountable_gap = _resolve_accountable(
   650	        root, accountable_flag, attribution)
   651	    if accountable_name is None:
   652	        return None, ("no accountable human could be established: no "
   653	                      "--accountable flag was given, the store named no "
   654	                      "human actor, and `git config user.name` returned "
   655	                      "nothing at %s" % root)
   656	
   657	    method_name, method_defaulted = _method_name(method_flag, decisions)
   658	    evidence_entries = _evidence_entries(evidence_pairs)
   659	
   660	    items = list(_default_gap_lines(tasks, evidence_pairs))
   661	    for gap in (files_gap, store_gap, accountable_gap):
   662	        if gap:
   663	            items.append(gap)
   664	    if evidence_entries:
   665	        items.append(
   666	            "evidence origin (build system vs laptop) is not tracked by "
   667	            "the BrotherMode store's own evidence table; every entry above "
   668	            "is reported as local until that distinction exists")
   669	    justification = None
   670	    if not items:
   671	        justification = ("every default gap category was found examined in "
   672	                         "the store's own task and evidence text, and no "
   673	                         "other gap was detected by this generator")
   674	
   675	    what_was_done = ["change in %s, commit range %s..%s" % (repo, base, head)]
   676	    if files:
   677	        what_was_done.append(
   678	            "%d file(s) touched: %s" % (len(files), ", ".join(files)))
   679	    else:
   680	        what_was_done.append(
   681	            "no files were touched between %s and %s" % (base, head))
   682	
   683	    who_did_it = ["accountable: %s" % accountable_name]
   684	    for session in sessions:
   685	        claims = ", ".join(session["claims"]) if session["claims"] else "(no claim named)"
   686	        who_did_it.append("session %s: claims %s" % (session["label"], claims))
   687	
   688	    if evidence_entries:
   689	        what_was_run = [
   690	            "`%s` returned %s (%s, %s)" % (
   691	                e["command"], e["verdict"], e["origin"], e["timestamp"])
   692	            for e in evidence_entries]
   693	    else:
   694	        what_was_run = [
   695	            "no verification evidence is recorded in the BrotherMode store "
   696	            "for project %r" % project_id]
   697	
   698	    where_it_came_from = [method_name]
   699	    what_was_not_established = items if items else [justification]
   700	
   701	    passport = {
   702	        "schema": GENERATE_SCHEMA_MARKER,
   703	        "generatedAt": now_str,
   704	        "whatWasDone": what_was_done,
   705	        "whoDidIt": who_did_it,
   706	        "whatWasRun": what_was_run,
   707	        "whatWasNotEstablished": what_was_not_established,
   708	        "whereItCameFrom": where_it_came_from,
   709	        "change": {
   710	            "repo": repo,
   711	            "baseCommit": base,
   712	            "headCommit": head,
   713	            "filesTouched": files,
   714	        },
   715	        "details": {
   716	            "who": {"sessions": sessions, "accountableHuman": accountable_name},
   717	            "evidence": evidence_entries,
   718	            "method": {"name": method_name},
   719	            "notEstablished": {"items": items, "noneClaimJustification": justification},
   720	        },
   721	    }
   722	    return passport, None

   725	def _usage_generate():
   726	    cmd = None
   727	    mod, _err = _load_bm_store()
   728	    if mod is not None:
   729	        try:
   730	            cmd = mod.invocation("bm_passport.py", __file__)
   731	        except Exception:
   732	            cmd = None
   733	    if not cmd:
   734	        cmd = "python3 %s" % os.path.abspath(__file__)
   735	    return ("Usage: %s generate --project-id ID --base SHA --head SHA\n"
   736	            "         [--accountable NAME] [--method NAME] [--now ISO8601]\n"
   737	            "         [--out PATH] [--root DIR]\n\n"
   738	            "Writes the full five-field change passport (schema/change-"
   739	            "passport.v1.json)\nto stdout, or to --out when given. Exit 0 "
   740	            "on a written passport, whatever it\ncould and could not "
   741	            "establish. Exit 1 when no accountable human could be\n"
   742	            "established, or when --out could not be written. Exit 2 for a "
   743	            "usage error\nor an unresolvable project root." % cmd)
   744	
   745	
   746	def _parse_generate_argv(argv):
   747	    """(args_dict_or_None, error_or_None). error == 'help' means print
   748	    generate usage and exit 0."""
   749	    values = {"project_id": None, "base": None, "head": None,
   750	             "accountable": None, "method": None, "now": None,
   751	             "out": None, "root": None}
   752	    flag_to_key = {
   753	        "--project-id": "project_id", "--base": "base", "--head": "head",
   754	        "--accountable": "accountable", "--method": "method",
   755	        "--now": "now", "--out": "out", "--root": "root",
   756	    }
   757	    args = list(argv)
   758	    i = 0
   759	    while i < len(args):
   760	        arg = args[i]
   761	        if arg in ("--help", "-h"):
   762	            return None, "help"
   763	        if arg in flag_to_key:
   764	            if i + 1 >= len(args):
   765	                return None, "bm_passport generate: %s requires a value" % arg
   766	            values[flag_to_key[arg]] = args[i + 1]
   767	            i += 2
   768	        else:
   769	            return None, "bm_passport generate: unknown argument: %s" % arg
   770	    missing = [flag for flag, key in
   771	              (("--project-id", "project_id"), ("--base", "base"), ("--head", "head"))
   772	              if not (values[key] or "").strip()]
   773	    if missing:
   774	        return None, ("bm_passport generate: missing required argument(s): "
   775	                      "%s" % ", ".join(missing))
   776	    return values, None
   777	
   778	
   779	def _run_generate(argv):
   780	    values, err = _parse_generate_argv(argv)
   781	    if err == "help":
   782	        sys.stdout.write(_usage_generate() + "\n")
   783	        return 0
   784	    if err:
   785	        sys.stderr.write(err + "\n")
   786	        sys.stderr.write(_usage_generate() + "\n")
   787	        return 2
   788	
   789	    root, reason = resolve_project_root(values["root"])
   790	    if root is None:
   791	        sys.stdout.write("NO-DATA: %s\n" % reason)
   792	        return 2
   793	
   794	    passport, error = build_passport(
   795	        root, values["project_id"], values["base"], values["head"],
   796	        values["accountable"], values["method"], values["now"])
   797	    if error:
   798	        sys.stderr.write("bm-passport generate: %s\n" % error)
   799	        return 1
   800	
   801	    text = json.dumps(passport, sort_keys=True, indent=2,
   802	                      ensure_ascii=False) + "\n"
   803	    if not values["out"]:
   804	        sys.stdout.write(text)
   805	        return 0
   806	    try:
   807	        out_path = values["out"]
   808	        parent = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(out_path))
   809	        if parent and not os.path.isdir(parent):
   810	            os.makedirs(parent)
   811	        tmp_path = out_path + ".tmp"
   812	        with open(tmp_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   813	            fh.write(text)
   814	        os.replace(tmp_path, out_path)
   815	    except OSError as e:
   816	        sys.stderr.write("bm-passport generate: could not write %s (%s)\n"
   817	                         % (values["out"], e))
   818	        return 1
   819	    return 0

   839	    if mod is not None:

/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba docs/PASSPORT.md | sed -n '1,220p'; find schema/fixtures -maxdepth 1 -type f -print | sort" in /Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	# The change passport
     2	
     3	The change passport is the one object that travels from BrotherMode
     4	(execution provenance) to BrotherSBE (assurance). It never travels back. If
     5	the assurance side needs something the passport does not carry, that is a
     6	defect in the passport, not permission to reach into execution state. Full
     7	spec authority: `docs/NORTH-STAR-CHAIN.md`, section "The change passport, in
     8	full".
     9	
    10	This page is the plain reading: what the five fields mean, the shape a
    11	producer actually writes, how to generate one, how to validate one on its
    12	own, and what this first version deliberately does not establish.
    13	
    14	## The five fields
    15	
    16	1. WHAT WAS DONE. The change identity, the commit range, the files touched.
    17	2. WHO DID IT. Which sessions wrote, which claims they held, and the human
    18	   accountable for the result, by name. Accountability is a name, never a
    19	   role.
    20	3. WHAT WAS RUN. Every verification executed after the last edit, its
    21	   result, and whether a build system or a laptop produced it.
    22	4. WHAT WAS NOT ESTABLISHED. The executing side's own honest list of what it
    23	   did not check. MANDATORY, never empty. Regression, performance,
    24	   cross-device, interface behaviour and translated copy belong here by
    25	   default unless something actually examined them.
    26	5. WHERE IT CAME FROM. The development method used, named and not judged.
    27	
    28	## The worked shape
    29	
    30	A passport is one JSON document. The five fields above sit at the top level
    31	as plain-language string lists, so a reader who only wants the fast version
    32	can read those five keys and stop:
    33	
    34	```json
    35	{
    36	  "schema": "change-passport/v1",
    37	  "generatedAt": "2026-08-20T10:00:00Z",
    38	  "whatWasDone": [
    39	    "change in <repo>, commit range <base>..<head>",
    40	    "1 file(s) touched: a.txt"
    41	  ],
    42	  "whoDidIt": [
    43	    "accountable: Fixture Person",
    44	    "session sess-xyz: claims Do the thing, upsert_project"
    45	  ],
    46	  "whatWasRun": [
    47	    "`python3 tools/test_all.py` returned green (local, 2026-08-20T09:30:00Z)"
    48	  ],
    49	  "whatWasNotEstablished": [
    50	    "no regression pass is recorded in the store for this change",
    51	    "..."
    52	  ],
    53	  "whereItCameFrom": [
    54	    "no method plugin detected; native flow"
    55	  ],
    56	  "change": { "repo": "...", "baseCommit": "...", "headCommit": "...", "filesTouched": ["a.txt"] },
    57	  "details": { "who": {...}, "evidence": [...], "method": {...}, "notEstablished": {...} }
    58	}
    59	```
    60	
    61	`change` and `details` are where the five top level fields come from: `change`
    62	holds the commit identity, `details` holds the session, evidence, method and
    63	gap records the five fields are derived from. The full, formal shape is
    64	`schema/change-passport.v1.json`, a JSON Schema draft 2020-12 document any
    65	third party can read without installing anything from this repository.
    66	
    67	## Generating one
    68	
    69	```
    70	python3 tools/bm_passport.py generate --project-id ID --base SHA --head SHA \
    71	    [--accountable NAME] [--method NAME] [--now ISO8601] [--out PATH]
    72	```
    73	
    74	Prints the passport to stdout by default, or writes it to `--out` when
    75	given. `--project-id`, `--base` and `--head` are required. The generator
    76	reads only public record surfaces: `tools/bm_store.py`'s own read accessors
    77	(`get_project`, `list_tasks`, `list_evidence`, `list_attribution`,
    78	`open_key_decisions`), plain `git` for the commit range and the files
    79	touched, and the accountable and method flags. It never reads raw SQLite and
    80	never reads the sentinel surface, which is private by design.
    81	
    82	Exit 0 on a written passport, whatever it could and could not establish.
    83	Exit 1 only when no accountable human could be established at all (no
    84	`--accountable`, nothing in the store, and `git config user.name` empty), or
    85	when `--out` could not be written. Exit 2 for a usage error or a project
    86	root that could not be resolved.
    87	
    88	DETERMINISM. Two generate calls against the same tree with the same `--now`
    89	produce byte identical output. Without `--now`, only `generatedAt` differs.
    90	Everything else, the sorted file list, the sorted session claims, the gap
    91	lines, is assembled in a fixed order rather than left to depend on
    92	iteration order.
    93	
    94	## Validating one, standalone
    95	
    96	```
    97	python3 tools/bm_passport_validator.py <file>
    98	```
    99	
   100	No dependency on this repository beyond the standard library, so a reader
   101	who never installed BrotherMode can still check a passport by hand. Prints
   102	`VALID` and exits 0 when every rule holds. Prints one named reason per line
   103	and exits 1 when the document is well formed JSON but breaks a rule. Exits 2
   104	with a named error when the file cannot be read or is not valid JSON.
   105	
   106	The rules: the `schema` key equals `change-passport/v1`; the five fields
   107	above are each present and answered, meaning a non-empty list of non-blank
   108	strings; field 4's own record (`details.notEstablished`) carries a non-empty
   109	`items` list, or an empty list paired with a non-blank
   110	`noneClaimJustification`, because a literal claim that nothing was left
   111	unestablished needs its own justification; `change.baseCommit` and
   112	`change.headCommit` are present and hex shaped; `details.who.accountableHuman`
   113	is a non-blank name; every entry in `details.evidence` carries an `origin` of
   114	`local` or `ci` and a `timestamp`.
   115	
   116	`schema/fixtures/` carries one valid document
   117	(`change-passport.v1.canonical.json`) and three invalid ones, each breaking
   118	exactly one rule by name: `invalid-hollow-field4.json`,
   119	`invalid-missing-accountable.json`, `invalid-evidence-no-origin.json`.
   120	
   121	## What v1 does not establish
   122	
   123	The fourth field is the honest list this producer could assemble from its
   124	own store and from git alone. Two limits sit above that list, worth stating
   125	once here rather than leaving a reader to discover them:
   126	
   127	Evidence origin. `details.evidence[].origin` is always `local` today,
   128	because BrotherMode's own evidence table records a check's command and note
   129	but not whether a build system ran it. Every entry generated by this tool
   130	carries a line in field 4 saying so, rather than guessing `ci` for anything.
   131	
   132	Trust of `ciRunId` on the consuming side is disclosure level only. BrotherSBE's
   133	own passport reader, `tools/sbe_passport.py`, states this about the value
   134	plainly in its own docstring: a truthy `ciRunId` means the receipt CLAIMS a
   135	build system produced it, never that one actually did, because
   136	`SBE_CI_RUN_ID` is an environment variable any local shell can export. A
   137	forged id reaches the same reading a real one would. This passport format
   138	inherits that same limit wherever a `ciRunId` appears; it is a claim, not
   139	proof.
   140	
   141	Not production ready beyond what the tests in `tools/test_bm_passport.py`
   142	actually exercise: a fixture store built through the same read accessors the
   143	generator uses, and the round trip against BrotherSBE's own consumer where
   144	that sibling repository is present on the machine running the suite.

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Standalone validator for a change passport v1 document
     3	(schema/change-passport.v1.json; docs/PASSPORT.md is the plain-language
     4	guide; docs/NORTH-STAR-CHAIN.md, section "The change passport, in full", is
     5	the spec authority).
     6	
     7	WHY STANDALONE. A third party (BrotherSBE, a CI runner, anyone who never
     8	installed this repository) needs to check a passport file without pulling in
     9	this project's own tooling or a jsonschema library. So this file imports
    10	NOTHING from this repository and NOTHING beyond the Python standard library.
    11	It re-implements the handful of rules that actually matter by hand, rather
    12	than deferring to the JSON Schema document, because a schema-only check
    13	cannot express "field 4's items may be empty only when a justification is
    14	given" without machinery this file is deliberately avoiding.
    15	
    16	WHAT IT CHECKS, each one a NAMED reason on its own line when it fails:
    17	
    18	  1. "schema" equals the literal string "change-passport/v1".
    19	  2. The five consumer keys (whatWasDone, whoDidIt, whatWasRun,
    20	     whatWasNotEstablished, whereItCameFrom) are present and ANSWERED: a
    21	     non-empty list whose entries are non-blank strings. Matches the
    22	     consuming side's own answered() (BrotherSBE tools/sbe_onepager.py):
    23	     '' and None carry nothing, an empty list carries nothing.
    24	  3. Field 4's law: details.notEstablished.items is non-empty, OR items is
    25	     empty and details.notEstablished.noneClaimJustification is a non-blank
    26	     string. A literal claim that nothing was left unestablished needs its
    27	     own justification.
    28	  4. change.baseCommit and change.headCommit are present and hex-shaped
    29	     (7 to 40 hex characters).
    30	  5. details.who.accountableHuman is a non-blank string. Accountability is a
    31	     name, never a role.
    32	  6. Every entry in details.evidence carries an origin in {"local", "ci"}
    33	     and a timestamp.
    34	
    35	Exit codes: 0 and the line "VALID" when every check passes. 1 and one line
    36	per violation when the document is well-formed JSON but fails a rule.
    37	2 and one line naming the problem when the file cannot be read or is not
    38	valid JSON. A crash is never a pass: any unexpected exception is caught and
    39	reported as a named error at exit 2.
    40	
    41	Python 3.9, standard library only, no third-party schema library.
    42	
    43	Usage: python3 tools/bm_passport_validator.py <file>

    45	import json
    46	import re
    47	import sys
    48	
    49	_COMMIT_RE = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-fA-F]{7,40}$")
    50	_SCHEMA_VALUE = "change-passport/v1"
    51	
    52	#: The five keys the consumer (BrotherSBE tools/sbe_passport.py) reads
    53	#: directly off the deposit, in the chain's own order.
    54	_CONSUMER_KEYS = (
    55	    (1, "whatWasDone"),
    56	    (2, "whoDidIt"),
    57	    (3, "whatWasRun"),
    58	    (4, "whatWasNotEstablished"),
    59	    (5, "whereItCameFrom"),
    60	)
    61	
    62	
    63	def _answered_string(value):
    64	    return isinstance(value, str) and value.strip() != ""
    65	
    66	
    67	def _answered_list_of_strings(value):
    68	    """True when `value` is a non-empty list whose every entry is a
    69	    non-blank string. Mirrors the consuming side's answered() for a list:
    70	    non-empty AND every element itself answered."""
    71	    if not isinstance(value, list) or not value:
    72	        return False
    73	    return all(_answered_string(v) for v in value)
    74	
    75	
    76	def validate(doc):

    78	    is assumed to already be parsed JSON (any type); every shape check is

   105	    # about the SOURCE record, not about whether the derived line happened
   106	    # to be non-empty.
   107	    not_established = details.get("notEstablished") if details_is_object else None
   108	    if not isinstance(not_established, dict):
   109	        problems.append(
   110	            "details.notEstablished must be an object, got %r"
   111	            % (not_established,))
   112	    else:
   113	        items = not_established.get("items")

   115	        if not isinstance(items, list):
   116	            problems.append(
   117	                "details.notEstablished.items must be a list, got %r"
   118	                % (items,))
   119	        elif not items:
   120	            if not _answered_string(justification):
   121	                problems.append(
   122	                    "details.notEstablished.items is empty and "
   123	                    "noneClaimJustification is not a non-blank string "
   124	                    "(%r); a literal claim that nothing was left "
   125	                    "unestablished needs its own justification"
   126	                    % (justification,))
   127	        elif not all(_answered_string(v) for v in items):
   128	            problems.append(
   129	                "details.notEstablished.items contains a blank or "
   130	                "non-string entry: %r" % (items,))
   131	
   132	    # 4. change identity: baseCommit / headCommit present and hex-shaped.
   133	    change = doc.get("change")
   134	    if not isinstance(change, dict):
   135	        problems.append("change must be an object, got %r" % (change,))
   136	    else:
   137	        for field in ("baseCommit", "headCommit"):
   138	            value = change.get(field)
   139	            if not _answered_string(value):
   140	                problems.append(
   141	                    "change.%s is required and must be a non-blank "
   142	                    "string, got %r" % (field, value))
   143	            elif not _COMMIT_RE.match(value.strip()):
   144	                problems.append(
   145	                    "change.%s is not hex-shaped (7 to 40 hex characters): "
   146	                    "%r" % (field, value))
   147	
   148	    # 5. accountableHuman: a name, never a role, never blank.
   149	    who = details.get("who") if details_is_object else None

   151	        problems.append(
   152	            "details.who must be an object, got %r" % (who,))
   153	    else:
   154	        accountable = who.get("accountableHuman")
   155	        if not _answered_string(accountable):
   156	            problems.append(
   157	                "details.who.accountableHuman is required and must be a "
   158	                "non-blank name, got %r" % (accountable,))
   159	
   160	    # 6. every evidence entry carries origin in {local, ci} and a timestamp.
   161	    evidence = details.get("evidence") if details_is_object else None
   162	    if not isinstance(evidence, list):
   163	        problems.append(
   164	            "details.evidence must be a list, got %r" % (evidence,))
   165	    else:
   166	        for index, entry in enumerate(evidence):
   167	            if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   168	                problems.append(
   169	                    "details.evidence[%d] is not an object: %r"
   170	                    % (index, entry))
   171	                continue

   175	                    "details.evidence[%d].origin must be \"local\" or "
   176	                    "\"ci\", got %r" % (index, origin))

   178	                problems.append(
   179	                    "details.evidence[%d].timestamp is required and must "
   180	                    "be a non-blank string, got %r"

   186	def _load(path):
   187	    """Return (doc, error_line_or_None). Never raises: every failure to
   188	    read or parse becomes a plain-English line the caller prints at exit 2,
   189	    because an unreadable file and an invalid document are different facts
   190	    and only the second one is worth exit 1's "here is what to fix"."""
   191	    try:
   192	        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   193	            text = fh.read()
   194	    except OSError as e:
   195	        return None, "could not read %s (%s: %s)" % (path, type(e).__name__, e)
   196	    try:
   197	        return json.loads(text), None
   198	    except ValueError as e:
   199	        return None, "%s is not valid JSON (%s)" % (path, e)
   200	
   201	
   202	def main(argv):
   203	    if len(argv) != 1:
   204	        sys.stderr.write(
   205	            "usage: python3 tools/bm_passport_validator.py <file>\n")
   206	        return 2
   207	    doc, error = _load(argv[0])
   208	    if error is not None:
   209	        sys.stdout.write(error + "\n")
   210	        return 2
   211	
   212	    problems = validate(doc)
   213	    if problems:
   214	        for line in problems:
   215	            sys.stdout.write(line + "\n")
   216	        return 1
   217	    sys.stdout.write("VALID\n")
   218	    return 0
   219	
   220	
   221	if __name__ == "__main__":
   222	    try:
   223	        sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))
   224	    except Exception as exc:
   225	        sys.stdout.write("%s: %s\n" % (type(exc).__name__, exc))
   226	        sys.exit(2)
