+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# `generate`: the full five field passport (schema/change-passport.v1.json).
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+def _git(args, cwd, env=None):
+    full_env = dict(os.environ, GIT_AUTHOR_NAME="t", GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL="t@example.com",
+                    GIT_COMMITTER_NAME="t", GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL="t@example.com")
+    if env:
+        full_env.update(env)
+    return subprocess.run(["git"] + list(args), cwd=cwd, env=full_env,
+                          stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
+                          universal_newlines=True, timeout=60)
+
+
+def make_repo(tmp):
+    """A two commit repository at `tmp`. Returns (base, head)."""
+    _git(["init", "-q"], tmp)
+    write_text(tmp, "a.txt", "one\n")
+    _git(["add", "-A"], tmp)
+    _git(["commit", "-q", "-m", "one"], tmp)
+    base = _git(["rev-parse", "HEAD"], tmp).stdout.strip()
+    write_text(tmp, "a.txt", "two\n")
+    _git(["add", "-A"], tmp)
+    _git(["commit", "-q", "-m", "two"], tmp)
+    head = _git(["rev-parse", "HEAD"], tmp).stdout.strip()
+    return base, head
+
+
+def make_project_fixture(tmp, project_id="proj-1", actor_name="Fixture Person",
+                         session_id="sess-xyz", with_evidence=True):
+    """A real, healthy store at `tmp` carrying one project, one task, and
+    (unless with_evidence is False) one evidence row, all written through
+    bm_store.py's own write API, never a hand written store file. Every
+    write shares one actor, so the attribution log names one session."""
+    mod = _load_bm_store_module()
+    store = mod.Store(tmp, create=True)
+    actor = {"actor_type": "human", "actor_name": actor_name,
+             "session_id": session_id}
+    try:
+        store.upsert_project({
+            "project_id": project_id, "name": "Fixture Project",
+            "created_at": "2026-08-20T09:00:00Z",
+            "updated_at": "2026-08-20T09:00:00Z",
+        }, actor)
+        task_id = store.create_task({
+            "task_id": "task-1", "project_id": project_id,
+            "title": "Do the thing", "status": "planned",
+        }, actor)
+        if with_evidence:
+            store.add_evidence({
+                "evidence_id": "ev-1", "subject_type": "task",
+                "subject_id": task_id, "kind": "test",
+                "ref": "python3 tools/test_all.py", "note": "green",
+                "created_at": "2026-08-20T09:30:00Z",
+            }, project_id, actor)
+    finally:
+        store.close()
+    return task_id
+
+
+def run_generate(*args, **kw):
+    env = dict(os.environ)
+    env.pop("BROTHERMODE_ROOT", None)
+    env.update(kw.get("env_over") or {})
+    return subprocess.run(
+        [sys.executable, TOOL_PATH, "generate"] + list(args),
+        stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
+        universal_newlines=True, cwd=ROOT, env=env)
+
+
+class GenerateRoundTripTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    """A fixture store built through the write API, read back by `generate`
+    through the five allowed accessors. Structural assertions, not exact
+    string equality, on everything that came out of the store: what
+    matters here is that a real name and a real command surface, not that
+    they land in one particular sentence shape a later refactor might
+    reword."""
+
+    def test_round_trip_carries_real_session_and_evidence_data(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
+            base, head = make_repo(tmp)
+            make_project_fixture(tmp)
+            result = run_generate("--root", tmp, "--project-id", "proj-1",
+                                  "--base", base, "--head", head,
+                                  "--now", "2026-08-20T10:00:00Z")
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
+        passport = json.loads(result.stdout)
+
+        self.assertEqual(passport["schema"], "change-passport/v1")
+        self.assertEqual(passport["change"]["baseCommit"], base)
+        self.assertEqual(passport["change"]["headCommit"], head)
+        self.assertEqual(passport["change"]["filesTouched"], ["a.txt"])
+
+        who = passport["details"]["who"]
+        self.assertEqual(who["accountableHuman"], "Fixture Person")
+        self.assertEqual(len(who["sessions"]), 1)
+        self.assertEqual(who["sessions"][0]["label"], "sess-xyz")
+        self.assertIn("Do the thing", who["sessions"][0]["claims"])
+
+        evidence = passport["details"]["evidence"]
+        self.assertEqual(len(evidence), 1)
+        self.assertEqual(evidence[0]["command"], "python3 tools/test_all.py")
+        self.assertEqual(evidence[0]["verdict"], "green")
+
+    def test_no_project_in_the_store_still_produces_a_passport(self):
+        """The store exists but the requested project does not: a named
+        gap, never a crash and never a refusal (the generator reports, it
+        does not block, on anything short of the accountable law)."""
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
+            base, head = make_repo(tmp)
+            make_project_fixture(tmp, project_id="some-other-project")
+            result = run_generate("--root", tmp, "--project-id", "missing",
+                                  "--base", base, "--head", head,
+                                  "--accountable", "Test Person")
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
+        passport = json.loads(result.stdout)
+        self.assertIn("no project", " ".join(passport["whatWasNotEstablished"]))
+
+
+class GenerateDeterminismTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_two_generates_with_the_same_now_are_byte_identical(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
+            base, head = make_repo(tmp)
+            make_project_fixture(tmp)
+            out1 = os.path.join(tmp, "one.json")
+            out2 = os.path.join(tmp, "two.json")
+            first = run_generate("--root", tmp, "--project-id", "proj-1",
+                                 "--base", base, "--head", head,
+                                 "--now", "2026-08-20T10:00:00Z", "--out", out1)
+            second = run_generate("--root", tmp, "--project-id", "proj-1",
+                                  "--base", base, "--head", head,
+                                  "--now", "2026-08-20T10:00:00Z", "--out", out2)
+            self.assertEqual(first.returncode, 0, first.stdout + first.stderr)
+            self.assertEqual(second.returncode, 0, second.stdout + second.stderr)
+            with io.open(out1, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
+                bytes1 = fh.read()
+            with io.open(out2, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
+                bytes2 = fh.read()
+        self.assertEqual(bytes1, bytes2,
+                         "two generate calls sharing the same --now produced "
+                         "different bytes")
+
+    def test_generatedAt_is_the_only_thing_that_differs_without_now(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
+            base, head = make_repo(tmp)
+            make_project_fixture(tmp)
+            first = run_generate("--root", tmp, "--project-id", "proj-1",
+                                 "--base", base, "--head", head)
+            second = run_generate("--root", tmp, "--project-id", "proj-1",
+                                  "--base", base, "--head", head)
+        self.assertEqual(first.returncode, 0, first.stdout + first.stderr)
+        self.assertEqual(second.returncode, 0, second.stdout + second.stderr)
+        p1, p2 = json.loads(first.stdout), json.loads(second.stdout)
+        del p1["generatedAt"]
+        del p2["generatedAt"]
+        self.assertEqual(p1, p2)
+
+
+class GenerateAccountableErrorPathTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_no_accountable_source_at_all_is_a_named_exit_1(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
+            base, head = make_repo(tmp)
+            result = run_generate("--root", tmp, "--project-id", "proj-1",
+                                  "--base", base, "--head", head,
+                                  env_over=_no_git_identity_env(tmp))
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 1, result.stdout + result.stderr)
+        self.assertIn("no accountable human could be established",
+                      result.stderr)
+
+    def test_missing_required_arguments_is_a_usage_error(self):
+        result = run_generate("--project-id", "x")
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stdout + result.stderr)
+        self.assertIn("missing required argument", result.stderr)
+
+
+class GenerateFieldFourNeverEmptyTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_field_four_is_never_empty_on_an_empty_store(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
+            base, head = make_repo(tmp)
+            result = run_generate("--root", tmp, "--project-id", "nothing-here",
+                                  "--base", base, "--head", head,
+                                  "--accountable", "Test Person")
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
+        passport = json.loads(result.stdout)
+        self.assertTrue(passport["whatWasNotEstablished"])
+        self.assertTrue(passport["details"]["notEstablished"]["items"])
+
+    def test_field_four_is_never_empty_on_a_full_store(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
+            base, head = make_repo(tmp)
+            make_project_fixture(tmp)
+            result = run_generate("--root", tmp, "--project-id", "proj-1",
+                                  "--base", base, "--head", head,
+                                  "--accountable", "Test Person")
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
+        passport = json.loads(result.stdout)
+        self.assertTrue(passport["whatWasNotEstablished"])
+
+
+class GenerateEvidenceShapeTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_every_evidence_entry_carries_origin_and_timestamp(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
+            base, head = make_repo(tmp)
+            make_project_fixture(tmp)
+            result = run_generate("--root", tmp, "--project-id", "proj-1",
+                                  "--base", base, "--head", head)
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
+        passport = json.loads(result.stdout)
+        evidence = passport["details"]["evidence"]
+        self.assertTrue(evidence, "the fixture carries one evidence row")
+        for entry in evidence:
+            self.assertIn(entry["origin"], ("local", "ci"))
+            self.assertTrue(entry["timestamp"])
+
+
+class ValidatorTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    """The standalone validator (tools/bm_passport_validator.py) against
+    the canonical fixture and each of the three fixtures that break
+    exactly one rule."""
+
+    def run_validator(self, path):
+        return subprocess.run(
+            [sys.executable, VALIDATOR_PATH, path],
+            stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
+            universal_newlines=True)
+
+    def test_canonical_fixture_is_valid(self):
+        result = self.run_validator(CANONICAL_FIXTURE)
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
+        self.assertEqual(result.stdout.strip(), "VALID")
+
+    def test_hollow_field_4_is_named(self):
+        result = self.run_validator(INVALID_FIXTURES["hollow field 4"])
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 1, result.stdout + result.stderr)
+        self.assertIn("notEstablished", result.stdout)
+
+    def test_missing_accountable_is_named(self):
+        result = self.run_validator(INVALID_FIXTURES["missing accountable"])
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 1, result.stdout + result.stderr)
+        self.assertIn("accountableHuman", result.stdout)
+
+    def test_evidence_with_no_origin_is_named(self):
+        result = self.run_validator(INVALID_FIXTURES["evidence with no origin"])
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 1, result.stdout + result.stderr)
+        self.assertIn("origin", result.stdout)
+
+    def test_unreadable_file_is_a_named_error_at_exit_2(self):
+        result = self.run_validator(
+            os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(), "bm-passport-no-such-file.json"))
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stdout + result.stderr)
+        self.assertIn("could not read", result.stdout)
+
+    def test_non_json_file_is_a_named_error_at_exit_2(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
+            path = write_text(tmp, "not-json.json", "{ this is not json")
+            result = self.run_validator(path)
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stdout + result.stderr)
+        self.assertIn("not valid JSON", result.stdout)
+
+
+class BrotherSBEContractProofTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    """Read only proof that the canonical fixture, deposited exactly where
+    the sibling repository expects it, makes BrotherSBE's own consumer
+    report fields 2 and 5 as CARRIED. Never writes into BROTHERSBE_ROOT:
+    the fixture store lives in a temp dir, and BrotherSBE's tool is only
+    ever invoked with --root pointed at that temp dir."""
+
+    def setUp(self):
+        sbe_passport = os.path.join(BROTHERSBE_ROOT, "tools", "sbe_passport.py")
+        if not os.path.isfile(sbe_passport):
+            self.skipTest(
+                "BrotherSBE is not present at %s on this machine, so the "
+                "contract proof cannot run; this is a skip, never a pass, "
+                "and never a fail" % sbe_passport)
+        self.sbe_passport = sbe_passport
+
+    def test_fields_two_and_five_are_carried_from_the_deposit(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
+            sbe_dir = os.path.join(tmp, ".sbe")
+            os.makedirs(sbe_dir)
+            # Minimal tasks.json, mirroring BrotherSBE's own
+            # tools/test_sbe_passport.py fixture shape: an empty task list
+            # is a legal, usable registry for this seam, since fields 2 and
+            # 5 come from the deposit alone, never from tasks.
+            with io.open(os.path.join(sbe_dir, "tasks.json"), "w",
+                         encoding="utf-8") as fh:
+                json.dump({"tasks": []}, fh)
+            with io.open(CANONICAL_FIXTURE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
+                canonical_bytes = fh.read()
+            with io.open(os.path.join(sbe_dir, "passport.json"), "w",
+                         encoding="utf-8") as fh:
+                fh.write(canonical_bytes)
+
+            result = subprocess.run(
+                [sys.executable, self.sbe_passport, "--root", tmp, "--json"],
+                stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
+                universal_newlines=True)
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
+        report = json.loads(result.stdout)
+        states = {f["number"]: f["state"] for f in report["fields"]}
+        self.assertEqual(states.get(2), "CARRIED",
+                         "field 2 (who did it) was not reported CARRIED: %s"
+                         % report)
+        self.assertEqual(states.get(5), "CARRIED",
+                         "field 5 (where it came from) was not reported "
+                         "CARRIED: %s" % report)
+
+
 if __name__ == "__main__":
     unittest.main()

codex
1. **F1 — BLOCKER — Raw store data crosses the assurance boundary without an egress policy.** [tools/bm_passport.py:619](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:619), [tools/bm_store.py:5416](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_store.py:5416), [tools/bm_store.py:13629](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_store.py:13629).  
   What breaks: all five accessors bypass secret scrubbing, path masking, digest withholding, and founder-text withholding. `dump(raw=True)` proves local read authority, not export authority; moreover, the old deposit only aggregates raw rows into counts/session labels, while `generate` exports titles, commands, notes, artifacts, and names. This is not defensible by default for a traveling artifact. The missing guardrail is a passport-specific egress allowlist plus explicit authorization for sensitive export.  
   Fix: use `export_column`/a passport projection by default, turn withheld values into named gaps, and require an explicit warned `--include-sensitive` mode for raw export.

2. **F2 — BLOCKER — The five store reads do not share one SQLite snapshot.** [tools/bm_passport.py:631](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:631), [tools/bm_passport.py:635](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:635), [tools/bm_store.py:17211](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_store.py:17211).  
   What breaks: `isolation_level=None` makes every accessor an independent autocommit read. A writer can commit between project, task, attribution, decision, and per-task evidence queries, producing a passport that never matched any actual database state.  
   Fix: wrap all accessor calls in one deferred read transaction exposed by `ReadOnlyStore`, committing only after all rows are materialized.

3. **F3 — MAJOR — Temporary store contention is converted into a successful degraded passport.** [tools/bm_passport.py:463](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:463), [tools/bm_passport.py:644](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:644), [tools/bm_store.py:17330](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_store.py:17330).  
   What breaks: an exclusive migration or other lock can time out, become `state="unreadable"`, and still yield exit 0. If locking happens after open, the same exception instead escapes to the broad `NO-DATA` exit 2 handler. Identical contention therefore has inconsistent outcomes. Ordinary WAL writers do not expose half-transactions, but they do not solve F2.  
   Fix: classify `db-busy` separately, retry with bounded backoff, then refuse generation rather than serializing temporary contention as missing provenance.

4. **F4 — BLOCKER — Git subprocesses inherit provenance-changing environment state.** [tools/bm_passport.py:416](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:416), [tools/bm_passport.py:269](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:269), [tools/test_bm_passport.py:584](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/test_bm_passport.py:584).  
   What breaks: inherited `GIT_DIR`, `GIT_WORK_TREE`, `GIT_CONFIG_*`, `HOME`, `url.*.insteadOf`, locale, and `PATH` can redirect reads, rewrite the repository URL, inject `user.name`, change diff behavior, or select another `git` executable. `git -C root` does not neutralize these. Tests inherit the same environment and therefore do not challenge the class.  
   Fix: run Git with a command-specific allowlisted environment, unset repository/config injection variables, pin locale/config behavior, and require explicit accountability rather than ambient identity.

5. **F5 — MAJOR — Locale and Unicode handling is not portable.** [tools/bm_passport.py:269](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:269), [tools/bm_passport.py:801](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:801), [tools/bm_passport.py:812](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:812).  
   What breaks: `_git_user_name` uses text mode without an encoding, so decoding depends on locale; `_run_git` assumes all Git output is UTF-8 although POSIX filenames/config values may contain arbitrary bytes. Default stdout can transcode non-ASCII JSON through a Windows or legacy locale encoding, while text-mode file writes produce CRLF on Windows.  
   Fix: capture Git bytes and apply an explicit reject/escape policy, emit stdout as UTF-8 bytes, and open output with `encoding="utf-8", newline="\n"`.

6. **F6 — BLOCKER — `generate` can emit a schema-invalid passport at exit 0.** [tools/bm_passport.py:770](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:770), [tools/bm_passport.py:709](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:709), [schema/change-passport.v1.json:161](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/schema/change-passport.v1.json:161).  
   What breaks: `--base` and `--head` are checked only for non-blank text, then copied verbatim. Values such as `x` cause a named Git gap but are still written, violating the schema’s 7–40 hexadecimal pattern.  
   Fix: resolve both arguments with `git rev-parse --verify <value>^{commit}`, emit canonical hashes, and self-validate before writing.

7. **F7 — BLOCKER — The standalone validator does not validate the declared schema.** [tools/bm_passport_validator.py:76](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport_validator.py:76), [schema/change-passport.v1.json:7](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/schema/change-passport.v1.json:7).  
   What breaks: it accepts documents missing `generatedAt`, `change.repo`, `change.filesTouched`, `details.method`, session structure, and nested additional-property constraints. A minimal document lacking those required fields returns no problems. It also never checks that the five readable fields agree with their claimed source records.  
   Fix: implement every schema constraint and derived-field consistency rule, or use a real draft-2020-12 validator and retain the extra field-4 rule.

8. **F8 — BLOCKER — JSON loading is parser-differential and unbounded.** [tools/bm_passport_validator.py:192](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport_validator.py:192), [tools/bm_passport_validator.py:197](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport_validator.py:197), [tools/bm_passport.py:560](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:560).  
   What breaks: `json.loads` silently keeps the last duplicate key and accepts non-standard `NaN`/`Infinity`; another assurance parser may interpret the same artifact differently. The validator reads unlimited bytes, while generation joins and duplicates unlimited store strings, allowing memory exhaustion and enormous diagnostic output.  
   Fix: reject duplicate keys and non-finite constants during parsing, impose document/string/list limits, and stream or bound producer inputs.

9. **F9 — MAJOR — An absent requested project still produces a formally valid passport.** [tools/bm_passport.py:638](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:638), [tools/test_bm_passport.py:684](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/test_bm_passport.py:684), [schema/change-passport.v1.json:49](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/schema/change-passport.v1.json:49).  
   What breaks: the question’s “only generic defaults” premise is not quite accurate—the missing project is named in field 4. Nevertheless, a typo still yields exit 0, and `change` contains no machine-readable `projectId`; orphan attribution can even populate identity for a nonexistent project.  
   Fix: refuse a missing project by default; if degraded reports are required, require `--allow-missing-project` and carry `change.projectId` explicitly.

10. **F10 — MAJOR — The determinism promise omits real inputs.** [docs/PASSPORT.md:88](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/docs/PASSPORT.md:88), [tools/bm_passport.py:432](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:432), [tools/test_bm_passport.py:700](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/test_bm_passport.py:700).  
    What breaks: the same working tree and `--now` can differ because of `.git/config`/global Git config, inherited Git variables, `user.name`, localized Git failure text, concurrent store commits, Git version/config, and Windows newline translation. The test runs sequentially in one inherited environment and compares decoded text, not raw bytes. SQLite row ordering and filesystem ordering **COULD NOT BREAK** determinism here: accessed rows have explicit tie-breakers and files/sessions/claims are sorted.  
    Fix: define the promise over an immutable store snapshot plus pinned Git/environment inputs, and compare raw output bytes across locale/platform cases.

11. **F11 — MAJOR — Two producers targeting the same output can corrupt or overwrite each other.** [tools/bm_passport.py:807](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:807), [tools/bm_passport.py:811](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:811), [tools/bm_passport.py:935](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:935).  
    What breaks: both the old and new paths use the shared predictable `<out>.tmp`. Two processes can truncate/write the same inode, one can keep modifying it after the other renames it, and one `os.replace` then fails. A pre-planted temp symlink can also redirect the write; raw output receives only umask-derived permissions.  
    Fix: use a unique same-directory `O_EXCL` temp at mode `0600`, flush/fsync, atomically replace, clean up, and lock/refuse concurrent writes to one destination.

12. **F12 — MINOR — Generated usage commands are not shell-quoted for installation paths.** [tools/bm_passport.py:734](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:734), [tools/bm_passport.py:845](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:845).  
    What breaks: the fallback command cannot be pasted when the installation path contains spaces or shell metacharacters. Shell command injection itself **COULD NOT BREAK**: subprocesses use argument arrays with `shell=False`.  
    Fix: render fallback commands with `shlex.join`/`shlex.quote`.

13. **F13 — MAJOR — The cross-family contract test silently disappears on other machines.** [tools/test_bm_passport.py:62](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/test_bm_passport.py:62), [tools/test_bm_passport.py:848](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/test_bm_passport.py:848).  
    What breaks: the consumer proof assumes `~/Documents/BrotherSBE` and skips elsewhere, so normal CI can merge without ever exercising the assurance consumer.  
    Fix: make the sibling checkout/path an explicit CI input and require the integration job rather than accepting a skip.

**Q4 — additive dispatch:** **COULD NOT BREAK** at [tools/bm_passport.py:953](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:953). `main` receives `sys.argv[1:]`, and only an exact first operand `generate` selects the new parser; no valid old invocation began with a bare positional word. Old/new corruption is possible only through the shared-output race in F11, not through parser shadowing.

Verification note: the four fixture-based validator tests passed; the full suite could not create temporary directories under this read-only execution environment.

8. **F8 — BLOCKER — JSON loading is parser-differential and unbounded.** [tools/bm_passport_validator.py:192](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport_validator.py:192), [tools/bm_passport_validator.py:197](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport_validator.py:197), [tools/bm_passport.py:560](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:560).  
   What breaks: `json.loads` silently keeps the last duplicate key and accepts non-standard `NaN`/`Infinity`; another assurance parser may interpret the same artifact differently. The validator reads unlimited bytes, while generation joins and duplicates unlimited store strings, allowing memory exhaustion and enormous diagnostic output.  
   Fix: reject duplicate keys and non-finite constants during parsing, impose document/string/list limits, and stream or bound producer inputs.

9. **F9 — MAJOR — An absent requested project still produces a formally valid passport.** [tools/bm_passport.py:638](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:638), [tools/test_bm_passport.py:684](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/test_bm_passport.py:684), [schema/change-passport.v1.json:49](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/schema/change-passport.v1.json:49).  
   What breaks: the question’s “only generic defaults” premise is not quite accurate—the missing project is named in field 4. Nevertheless, a typo still yields exit 0, and `change` contains no machine-readable `projectId`; orphan attribution can even populate identity for a nonexistent project.  
   Fix: refuse a missing project by default; if degraded reports are required, require `--allow-missing-project` and carry `change.projectId` explicitly.

10. **F10 — MAJOR — The determinism promise omits real inputs.** [docs/PASSPORT.md:88](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/docs/PASSPORT.md:88), [tools/bm_passport.py:432](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:432), [tools/test_bm_passport.py:700](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/test_bm_passport.py:700).  
    What breaks: the same working tree and `--now` can differ because of `.git/config`/global Git config, inherited Git variables, `user.name`, localized Git failure text, concurrent store commits, Git version/config, and Windows newline translation. The test runs sequentially in one inherited environment and compares decoded text, not raw bytes. SQLite row ordering and filesystem ordering **COULD NOT BREAK** determinism here: accessed rows have explicit tie-breakers and files/sessions/claims are sorted.  
    Fix: define the promise over an immutable store snapshot plus pinned Git/environment inputs, and compare raw output bytes across locale/platform cases.

11. **F11 — MAJOR — Two producers targeting the same output can corrupt or overwrite each other.** [tools/bm_passport.py:807](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:807), [tools/bm_passport.py:811](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:811), [tools/bm_passport.py:935](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:935).  
    What breaks: both the old and new paths use the shared predictable `<out>.tmp`. Two processes can truncate/write the same inode, one can keep modifying it after the other renames it, and one `os.replace` then fails. A pre-planted temp symlink can also redirect the write; raw output receives only umask-derived permissions.  
    Fix: use a unique same-directory `O_EXCL` temp at mode `0600`, flush/fsync, atomically replace, clean up, and lock/refuse concurrent writes to one destination.

12. **F12 — MINOR — Generated usage commands are not shell-quoted for installation paths.** [tools/bm_passport.py:734](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:734), [tools/bm_passport.py:845](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:845).  
    What breaks: the fallback command cannot be pasted when the installation path contains spaces or shell metacharacters. Shell command injection itself **COULD NOT BREAK**: subprocesses use argument arrays with `shell=False`.  
    Fix: render fallback commands with `shlex.join`/`shlex.quote`.

13. **F13 — MAJOR — The cross-family contract test silently disappears on other machines.** [tools/test_bm_passport.py:62](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/test_bm_passport.py:62), [tools/test_bm_passport.py:848](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/test_bm_passport.py:848).  
    What breaks: the consumer proof assumes `~/Documents/BrotherSBE` and skips elsewhere, so normal CI can merge without ever exercising the assurance consumer.  
    Fix: make the sibling checkout/path an explicit CI input and require the integration job rather than accepting a skip.

**Q4 — additive dispatch:** **COULD NOT BREAK** at [tools/bm_passport.py:953](/Users/khalil.maaouni/Documents/BrotherModeUp/tools/bm_passport.py:953). `main` receives `sys.argv[1:]`, and only an exact first operand `generate` selects the new parser; no valid old invocation began with a bare positional word. Old/new corruption is possible only through the shared-output race in F11, not through parser shadowing.
